`backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/sqlite/introspection.py`:
```python
import sqlite3

from ...domain.errors import NotFound, ValidationFailed
from ...domain.metadata import ColumnInfo, Folder, NodeKind, ObjectDetail, ObjectNode, PathSegment, make_node
from ..sql_text import quote_ident_sqlite as q

_TYPE_FOLDERS: dict[Folder, str] = {
    Folder.TABLES: "table",
    Folder.VIEWS: "view",
    Folder.INDEXES: "index",
    Folder.TRIGGERS: "trigger",
}
_KIND_OF_TYPE = {
    "table": NodeKind.TABLE,
    "view": NodeKind.VIEW,
    "index": NodeKind.INDEX,
    "trigger": NodeKind.TRIGGER,
}


def _master(schema: str) -> str:
    return f"{q(schema)}.sqlite_master"


def _schemas(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[str]:
    return [row[1] for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA database_list") if row[1] != "temp"]


def columns(conn: sqlite3.Connection, schema: str, table: str) -> list[ColumnInfo]:
    rows = conn.execute(f"PRAGMA {q(schema)}.table_xinfo({q(table)})").fetchall()
    return [
        ColumnInfo(name=name, type_name=type_ or "", nullable=not notnull, default=default, is_primary_key=pk > 0)
        for _cid, name, type_, notnull, default, pk, _hidden in rows
    ]


def _table_indexes(conn: sqlite3.Connection, schema: str, table: str) -> list[tuple[str, bool]]:
    rows = conn.execute(f"PRAGMA {q(schema)}.index_list({q(table)})").fetchall()
    return sorted((row[1], bool(row[2])) for row in rows)


def _table_triggers(conn: sqlite3.Connection, schema: str, table: str) -> list[str]:
    rows = conn.execute(
        f"SELECT name FROM {_master(schema)} WHERE type = 'trigger' AND tbl_name = ? ORDER BY name", (table,)
    ).fetchall()
    return [row[0] for row in rows]


def _column_detail(col: ColumnInfo) -> str:
    parts = [col.type_name or "ANY"]
    if not col.nullable:
        parts.append("NOT NULL")
    if col.is_primary_key:
        parts.append("PK")
    return " ".join(parts)


def list_children(conn: sqlite3.Connection, path: list[PathSegment]) -> list[ObjectNode]:
    depth = len(path)
    if depth == 0:
        return [make_node(path, NodeKind.SCHEMA, s, has_children=True) for s in _schemas(conn)]
    schema = path[0].name
    last = path[-1]
    if depth == 1:
        counts = dict(conn.execute(f"SELECT type, count(*) FROM {_master(schema)} GROUP BY type").fetchall())
        return [
            make_node(path, NodeKind.FOLDER, folder, has_children=True)
            for folder, obj_type in _TYPE_FOLDERS.items()
            if counts.get(obj_type)
        ]
    if depth == 2 and last.kind == NodeKind.FOLDER:
        obj_type = _TYPE_FOLDERS[Folder(last.name)]
        kind = _KIND_OF_TYPE[obj_type]
        rows = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT name, tbl_name FROM {_master(schema)} WHERE type = ? ORDER BY name", (obj_type,)
        ).fetchall()
        return [
            make_node(
                path, kind, name,
                has_children=kind in (NodeKind.TABLE, NodeKind.VIEW),
                system=name.startswith("sqlite_"),
                detail=f"on {tbl}" if kind in (NodeKind.INDEX, NodeKind.TRIGGER) else None,
            )
            for name, tbl in rows
        ]
    if depth == 3 and last.kind in (NodeKind.TABLE, NodeKind.VIEW):
        folders = [Folder.COLUMNS]
        if last.kind == NodeKind.TABLE:
            if _table_indexes(conn, schema, last.name):
                folders.append(Folder.INDEXES)
            if _table_triggers(conn, schema, last.name):
                folders.append(Folder.TRIGGERS)
        return [make_node(path, NodeKind.FOLDER, f, has_children=True) for f in folders]
    if depth == 4 and last.kind == NodeKind.FOLDER:
        table = path[2].name
        if last.name == Folder.COLUMNS:
            return [
                make_node(path, NodeKind.COLUMN, c.name, has_children=False, detail=_column_detail(c))
                for c in columns(conn, schema, table)
            ]
        if last.name == Folder.INDEXES:
            return [
                make_node(
                    path, NodeKind.INDEX, name, has_children=False,
                    system=name.startswith("sqlite_autoindex"), detail="unique" if unique else None,
                )
                for name, unique in _table_indexes(conn, schema, table)
            ]
        if last.name == Folder.TRIGGERS:
            return [make_node(path, NodeKind.TRIGGER, n, has_children=False) for n in _table_triggers(conn, schema, table)]
    return []


def object_detail(conn: sqlite3.Connection, path: list[PathSegment]) -> ObjectDetail:
    if not path:
        raise ValidationFailed("Empty path")
    schema, last = path[0].name, path[-1]
    if last.kind in (NodeKind.TABLE, NodeKind.VIEW, NodeKind.INDEX, NodeKind.TRIGGER):
        row = conn.execute(f"SELECT sql FROM {_master(schema)} WHERE name = ?", (last.name,)).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise NotFound(f"{last.kind} '{last.name}' not found")
        ddl = f"{row[0]};" if row[0] else None
        cols = columns(conn, schema, last.name) if last.kind in (NodeKind.TABLE, NodeKind.VIEW) else None
        return ObjectDetail(path=path, kind=last.kind, name=last.name, ddl=ddl, columns=cols)
    if last.kind == NodeKind.COLUMN and len(path) >= 3:
        table = path[2].name
        match = [c for c in columns(conn, schema, table) if c.name == last.name]
        if not match:
            raise NotFound(f"Column '{last.name}' not found")
        return ObjectDetail(path=path, kind=last.kind, name=last.name, columns=match)
    raise ValidationFailed(f"No details available for {last.kind}")
```

`backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/sqlite/adapter.py`:
```python
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import quote, unquote, urlparse

from loguru import logger

from ...domain.errors import ConnectionFailed, ValidationFailed
from ...domain.execution import ColumnCategory, ColumnMeta, ServerMessage
from ...domain.metadata import ObjectDetail, ObjectNode, PathSegment, ServerInfo
from ...domain.profiles import AnyProfile, Engine, SqliteProfile, SqliteProfileDraft
from ..base import (
    DatabaseAdapter,
    DatabaseConnection,
    Endpoint,
    ParsedUrl,
    StatementCancelled,
    StatementError,
    StatementResult,
)
from ..values import encode_row
from . import introspection

_PEEK_ROWS = 200


def _translate(exc: sqlite3.Error) -> StatementError:
    message = str(exc)
    if isinstance(exc, sqlite3.OperationalError) and message == "interrupted":
        return StatementCancelled()
    return StatementError(message, code=getattr(exc, "sqlite_errorname", None))


def _category(value: Any) -> tuple[str, ColumnCategory]:
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return ("INTEGER" if isinstance(value, int) else "REAL"), ColumnCategory.NUMBER
    if isinstance(value, bytes):
        return "BLOB", ColumnCategory.BINARY
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return "TEXT", ColumnCategory.TEXT
    return "", ColumnCategory.OTHER


class SqliteStatementResult(StatementResult):
    def __init__(self, cursor: sqlite3.Cursor) -> None:
        self._cursor = cursor
        self._buffer: list[tuple[Any, ...]] = []
        self._columns: list[ColumnMeta] | None = None
        self._closed = False
        if cursor.description is not None:
            try:
                self._buffer = cursor.fetchmany(_PEEK_ROWS)
            except sqlite3.Error as exc:
                raise _translate(exc) from exc
            self._columns = [self._column(i, d[0]) for i, d in enumerate(cursor.description)]

    def _column(self, index: int, name: str) -> ColumnMeta:
        sample = next((row[index] for row in self._buffer if row[index] is not None), None)
        type_name, category = _category(sample)
        return ColumnMeta(name=name, type_name=type_name, category=category)

    def columns(self) -> list[ColumnMeta] | None:
        return self._columns

    def fetch(self, n: int) -> list[list[Any]]:
        if self._columns is None:
            return []
        rows = self._buffer[:n]
        self._buffer = self._buffer[n:]
        if len(rows) < n and not self._closed:
            try:
                rows.extend(self._cursor.fetchmany(n - len(rows)))
            except sqlite3.Error as exc:
                raise _translate(exc) from exc
        return [encode_row(r) for r in rows]

    def affected_rows(self) -> int | None:
        if self._columns is None and self._cursor.rowcount >= 0:
            return self._cursor.rowcount
        return None

    def next_result(self) -> bool:
        return False

    def take_messages(self) -> list[ServerMessage]:
        return []

    def close(self) -> None:
        if not self._closed:
            self._closed = True
            self._buffer = []
            self._cursor.close()


class SqliteConnection(DatabaseConnection):
    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, path: Path) -> None:
        self._conn = conn
        self._path = path

    def server_info(self) -> ServerInfo:
        return ServerInfo(engine=Engine.SQLITE, version=sqlite3.sqlite_version, current_database="main")

    def current_database(self) -> str | None:
        return "main"

    def list_children(self, path: list[PathSegment]) -> list[ObjectNode]:
        return introspection.list_children(self._conn, path)

    def object_detail(self, path: list[PathSegment]) -> ObjectDetail:
        return introspection.object_detail(self._conn, path)

    def run(self, sql: str) -> StatementResult:
        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
        try:
            cursor.execute(sql)
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            cursor.close()
            raise _translate(exc) from exc
        return SqliteStatementResult(cursor)

    def in_transaction(self) -> bool:
        return self._conn.in_transaction

    def cancel(self) -> None:
        self._conn.interrupt()

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._conn.close()
        logger.info("Closed SQLite database {}", self._path)


class SqliteAdapter(DatabaseAdapter):
    engine = Engine.SQLITE
    url_schemes = ("sqlite",)
    default_port = None

    def parse_url(self, url: str) -> ParsedUrl:
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        path = unquote(parsed.netloc + parsed.path)
        if not path:
            raise ValidationFailed("A SQLite URL needs a file path, e.g. sqlite:///Users/me/app.db")
        name = Path(path).name or "SQLite"
        return ParsedUrl(profile=SqliteProfileDraft(engine="sqlite", name=name, path=path))

    def open(
        self, profile: AnyProfile, endpoint: Endpoint | None, password: str | None, database: str | None
    ) -> DatabaseConnection:
        if not isinstance(profile, SqliteProfile):
            raise ValidationFailed("Not a SQLite profile")
        path = Path(profile.path).expanduser()
        if not path.is_file():
            raise ConnectionFailed(f"SQLite file not found: {path}")
        mode = "ro" if profile.read_only else "rw"
        uri = f"file:{quote(str(path))}?mode={mode}"
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(uri, uri=True, isolation_level=None, check_same_thread=True)
            conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            raise ConnectionFailed(f"Cannot open {path}: {exc}") from exc
        logger.info("Opened SQLite database {} (mode={})", path, mode)
        return SqliteConnection(conn, path)
```

### Task 7: PostgreSQL adapter — connection and execution

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/__init__.py`, `backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/loaders.py`, `backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/adapter.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_postgres_adapter.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 contract and helpers (`offset_to_line_col`, `first_keyword`, `encode_row`); `ServerProfile`, `ServerProfileDraft`, `SslConfig` (Task 3); `ConnectionFailed`, `ValidationFailed` (Task 1); `tests/support.py` (Task 5).
- Produces: `PostgresAdapter` (engine `postgres`, schemes `("postgres", "postgresql")`, `default_port=5432`, `capabilities.per_database_connection=True`); `PostgresConnection` with `column_meta(description)`, `drain_messages()`; module functions `configure_user_loaders(adapters)`, `category_for_oid(oid) -> ColumnCategory`, `translate_error(exc, sql) -> StatementError`. Introspection methods (`list_children`, `object_detail`) delegate to modules created in Task 8; in this task they raise `NotImplementedError`.

Notes for the implementer:
- psycopg's `cursor.stream()` raises `ProgrammingError("... didn't produce a result")` for statements that return no rows, and it only accepts one statement. So `run()` picks a path by the first keyword: `select/with/values/table/show/explain/fetch/(` or a `RETURNING` clause → streaming; everything else → `cursor.execute()` (simple protocol; supports several statements and `nextset()`).
- `stream()` holds the connection lock while the generator is suspended. A paused result must be `close()`d before running anything else on the same connection; `SessionWorker` (Task 10) guarantees that.
- Text-first loading is registered **per user cursor** (`cursor.adapters`), so introspection queries keep native Python types.
- When a streamed SELECT returns zero rows psycopg exposes no description; describe the statement through libpq (`pgconn.prepare` + `describe_prepared` on the unnamed statement) so the UI still gets column headers.

`backend/tests/test_postgres_adapter.py`:
```python
import threading
import time

import pytest

from dbaguy_backend.adapters.base import Endpoint, StatementCancelled, StatementError
from dbaguy_backend.adapters.postgres.adapter import PostgresAdapter
from dbaguy_backend.domain.errors import ConnectionFailed
from tests.support import PG_HOST, PG_PASSWORD, PG_PORT, make_pg_profile, requires_pg

pytestmark = requires_pg


@pytest.fixture
def conn():
    connection = PostgresAdapter().open(make_pg_profile(), Endpoint(PG_HOST, PG_PORT), PG_PASSWORD, None)
    yield connection
    connection.close()


def test_wrong_password():
    with pytest.raises(ConnectionFailed, match="password authentication failed"):
        PostgresAdapter().open(make_pg_profile(), Endpoint(PG_HOST, PG_PORT), "wrong", None)


def test_server_info(conn):
    info = conn.server_info()
    assert info.version.startswith("PostgreSQL 17")
    assert info.current_database == "dbaguy"


def test_stream_big_table_in_pages(conn):
    result = conn.run("SELECT id, label, value FROM big_table ORDER BY id")
    columns = result.columns()
    assert [c.name for c in columns] == ["id", "label", "value"]
    assert [c.category for c in columns] == ["number", "text", "number"]
    first = result.fetch(500)
    assert len(first) == 500 and first[0] == [1, "row 1", 1.5]
    total = len(first)
    while batch := result.fetch(5000):
        total += len(batch)
    assert total == 100_000
    result.close()


def test_text_first_values(conn):
    row = conn.run("SELECT huge_int, big_number, data FROM blobs").fetch(1)[0]
    assert row == ["9007199254740993", "12345678901234567890.123456789", {"$type": "bytes", "size": 4, "preview": "deadbeef"}]
    profile, tags, address = conn.run("SELECT profile, tags, address FROM customers WHERE id = 1").fetch(1)[0]
    assert '"score": 12345678901234567890' in profile
    assert tags == "{math,pioneer}"
    assert address == '("12 Analytical St",London,N1)'
    assert conn.run("SELECT name, address FROM customers WHERE email = 'empty@example.com'").fetch(1) == [["", None]]
    assert conn.run("SELECT name FROM customers WHERE id = 2").fetch(1) == [["林小明"]]
    statuses = conn.run("SELECT status FROM sales.orders ORDER BY id")
    assert statuses.columns()[0].type_name == "order_status"
    assert statuses.fetch(5) == [["paid"], ["pending"], ["shipped"]]


def test_dml_ddl_notice_and_vacuum(conn):
    assert conn.run("CREATE TEMP TABLE t_tmp (id int)").columns() is None
    assert conn.run("INSERT INTO t_tmp SELECT generate_series(1, 3)").affected_rows() == 3
    result = conn.run("DO $$ BEGIN RAISE NOTICE 'hello %', 42; END $$")
    assert any("hello 42" in m.text for m in result.take_messages())
    assert conn.run("VACUUM t_tmp").columns() is None


def test_multiple_statements_buffered(conn):
    result = conn.run("CREATE TEMP TABLE m (x int); INSERT INTO m VALUES (1), (2)")
    assert result.columns() is None
    assert result.next_result() is True
    assert result.affected_rows() == 2
    assert result.next_result() is False


def test_zero_row_select_still_has_columns(conn):
    result = conn.run("SELECT id, label FROM big_table WHERE false")
    assert [c.name for c in result.columns()] == ["id", "label"]
    assert result.fetch(10) == []


def test_select_into_is_not_an_error(conn):
    assert conn.run("SELECT 1 AS x INTO TEMP TABLE sel_into").columns() is None
    assert conn.run("SELECT x FROM sel_into").fetch(1) == [[1]]


def test_error_position_with_cjk(conn):
    sql = "SELECT '中文' AS a,\n  nope_column FROM big_table"
    with pytest.raises(StatementError) as info:
        conn.run(sql).fetch(1)
    assert info.value.code == "42703"
    assert (info.value.position.line, info.value.position.column) == (2, 3)


def test_cancel_running_query(conn):
    threading.Timer(0.5, conn.cancel).start()
    started = time.monotonic()
    with pytest.raises(StatementCancelled):
        conn.run("SELECT pg_sleep(30)").fetch(1)
    assert time.monotonic() - started < 5
    assert conn.run("SELECT 1 AS one").fetch(1) == [[1]]


def test_discard_stream_then_reuse(conn):
    result = conn.run("SELECT * FROM big_table")
    assert len(result.fetch(10)) == 10
    result.close()
    assert conn.run("SELECT 2").fetch(1) == [[2]]


def test_transaction_state(conn):
    conn.run("BEGIN")
    assert conn.in_transaction()
    conn.run("ROLLBACK")
    assert not conn.in_transaction()


def test_parse_url():
    parsed = PostgresAdapter().parse_url("postgresql://me:p%40ss@db.example.com:6543/app?sslmode=require&connect_timeout=5")
    draft = parsed.profile
    assert (draft.host, draft.port, draft.database, draft.username) == ("db.example.com", 6543, "app", "me")
    assert draft.ssl.mode == "require"
    assert draft.options == {"connect_timeout": "5"}
    assert parsed.password == "p@ss"
```

`backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/loaders.py`:
```python
from psycopg import postgres
from psycopg.adapt import AdaptersMap
from psycopg.types.string import TextLoader

from ...domain.execution import ColumnCategory

# Decoded natively by psycopg; every other type is loaded as the server's text representation,
# which keeps big numbers exact, keeps JSON text intact and survives values Python cannot
# represent (infinity, BC dates).
_NATIVE = frozenset({"bool", "int2", "int4", "float4", "float8", "bytea", "text", "varchar", "bpchar", "name", "char"})

_NAME_CATEGORY: dict[str, ColumnCategory] = {
    "bool": ColumnCategory.BOOL,
    **dict.fromkeys(("int2", "int4", "int8", "float4", "float8", "numeric", "oid", "money"), ColumnCategory.NUMBER),
    **dict.fromkeys(("date", "time", "timetz", "timestamp", "timestamptz", "interval"), ColumnCategory.DATETIME),
    "bytea": ColumnCategory.BINARY,
    **dict.fromkeys(("json", "jsonb"), ColumnCategory.JSON),
    **dict.fromkeys(("text", "varchar", "bpchar", "name", "char", "uuid", "xml"), ColumnCategory.TEXT),
}

_OID_CATEGORY: dict[int, ColumnCategory] = {
    info.oid: _NAME_CATEGORY[info.name] for info in postgres.types if info.name in _NAME_CATEGORY
}


def configure_user_loaders(adapters: AdaptersMap) -> None:
    """Register text loaders on a user-query cursor's adapters."""
    for info in postgres.types:
        if info.name not in _NATIVE:
            adapters.register_loader(info.oid, TextLoader)
        if info.array_oid:
            adapters.register_loader(info.array_oid, TextLoader)


def category_for_oid(oid: int) -> ColumnCategory:
    return _OID_CATEGORY.get(oid, ColumnCategory.OTHER)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the adapter**

`backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/adapter.py`:
```python
import os
import re
from collections import deque
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import parse_qsl, unquote, urlparse

import psycopg
from loguru import logger
from psycopg import postgres, pq

from ...domain.errors import ConnectionFailed, ValidationFailed
from ...domain.execution import ColumnMeta, ErrorPosition, MessageSeverity, ServerMessage
from ...domain.metadata import ObjectDetail, ObjectNode, PathSegment, ServerInfo
from ...domain.profiles import AnyProfile, Engine, ServerProfile, ServerProfileDraft, SslConfig
from ..base import (
    AdapterCapabilities,
    DatabaseAdapter,
    DatabaseConnection,
    Endpoint,
    ParsedUrl,
    StatementCancelled,
    StatementError,
    StatementResult,
)
from ..positions import offset_to_line_col
from ..sql_text import first_keyword
from ..values import encode_row
from .loaders import category_for_oid, configure_user_loaders

_ROW_KEYWORDS = frozenset({"select", "with", "values", "table", "show", "explain", "fetch", "("})
_RETURNING = re.compile(r"\breturning\b", re.IGNORECASE)
_STREAM_CHUNK = 200
_SSL_QUERY_KEYS = {"sslmode": "mode", "sslrootcert": "ca_file", "sslcert": "cert_file", "sslkey": "key_file"}

_CUSTOM_TYPES_SQL = """
SELECT t.oid, format_type(t.oid, NULL)
FROM pg_type t
WHERE t.typnamespace NOT IN ('pg_catalog'::regnamespace, 'information_schema'::regnamespace)
  AND t.typtype IN ('b', 'c', 'd', 'e', 'm', 'r')
  AND (t.typtype <> 'c' OR (SELECT c.relkind FROM pg_class c WHERE c.oid = t.typrelid) = 'c')
LIMIT 20000
"""


def translate_error(exc: psycopg.Error, sql: str) -> StatementError:
    diag = exc.diag
    if isinstance(exc, psycopg.errors.QueryCanceled):
        return StatementCancelled(diag.message_primary or "Query cancelled")
    message = diag.message_primary or str(exc).strip()
    if diag.message_detail:
        message += f"\nDETAIL: {diag.message_detail}"
    if diag.message_hint:
        message += f"\nHINT: {diag.message_hint}"
    position = None
    if diag.statement_position:
        line, column = offset_to_line_col(sql, int(diag.statement_position))
        position = ErrorPosition(line=line, column=column)
    return StatementError(message, code=diag.sqlstate, position=position)


def _likely_returns_rows(sql: str) -> bool:
    return first_keyword(sql) in _ROW_KEYWORDS or bool(_RETURNING.search(sql))


def _ssl_params(ssl: SslConfig) -> dict[str, str]:
    params = {"sslmode": ssl.mode}
    if ssl.ca_file:
        params["sslrootcert"] = os.path.expanduser(ssl.ca_file)
    elif ssl.mode in ("verify-ca", "verify-full"):
        params["sslrootcert"] = "system"  # libpq 16+: use the system trust store
    if ssl.cert_file:
        params["sslcert"] = os.path.expanduser(ssl.cert_file)
    if ssl.key_file:
        params["sslkey"] = os.path.expanduser(ssl.key_file)
    return params


class _BufferedResult(StatementResult):
    """Result of cursor.execute(): small results, DML/DDL, multi-statement scripts."""

    def __init__(self, owner: "PostgresConnection", cursor: psycopg.Cursor, sql: str) -> None:
        self._owner, self._cursor, self._sql = owner, cursor, sql

    def columns(self) -> list[ColumnMeta] | None:
        desc = self._cursor.description
        return self._owner.column_meta(desc) if desc else None

    def fetch(self, n: int) -> list[list[Any]]:
        if self._cursor.description is None:
            return []
        try:
            return [encode_row(r) for r in self._cursor.fetchmany(n)]
        except psycopg.Error as exc:
            raise translate_error(exc, self._sql) from exc

    def affected_rows(self) -> int | None:
        if self._cursor.description is None and self._cursor.rowcount >= 0:
            return self._cursor.rowcount
        return None

    def next_result(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self._cursor.nextset())

    def take_messages(self) -> list[ServerMessage]:
        return self._owner.drain_messages()

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._cursor.close()


class _StreamResult(StatementResult):
    """Row-returning statement streamed with bounded memory (libpq chunked mode)."""

    def __init__(self, owner: "PostgresConnection", cursor: psycopg.Cursor, sql: str) -> None:
        self._owner, self._cursor, self._sql = owner, cursor, sql
        self._gen = cursor.stream(sql, size=_STREAM_CHUNK)
        self._pending: list[Any] = []
        self._done = False
        self._closed = False
        self._columns: list[ColumnMeta] | None = None
        try:
            first = next(self._gen, None)
        except psycopg.ProgrammingError as exc:
            if "didn't produce a result" not in str(exc):
                self.close()
                raise translate_error(exc, sql) from exc
            first = None  # e.g. SELECT ... INTO: ran fine, returns no rows
            self._done = True
            return
        except psycopg.Error as exc:
            self.close()
            raise translate_error(exc, sql) from exc
        if first is None:
            self._done = True
            self._columns = owner.describe(sql)
        else:
            self._pending.append(first)
            self._columns = owner.column_meta(cursor.description)

    def columns(self) -> list[ColumnMeta] | None:
        return self._columns

    def fetch(self, n: int) -> list[list[Any]]:
        rows = self._pending[:n]
        self._pending = self._pending[n:]
        while len(rows) < n and not self._done:
            try:
                rows.append(next(self._gen))
            except StopIteration:
                self._done = True
            except psycopg.Error as exc:
                self._done = True
                raise translate_error(exc, self._sql) from exc
        return [encode_row(r) for r in rows]

    def affected_rows(self) -> int | None:
        return None

    def next_result(self) -> bool:
        return False

    def take_messages(self) -> list[ServerMessage]:
        return self._owner.drain_messages()

    def close(self) -> None:
        if self._closed:
            return
        self._closed = True
        self._pending = []
        try:
            self._gen.close()  # psycopg cancels and drains the rest
        except psycopg.Error as exc:
            logger.warning("Error while closing PostgreSQL stream: {}", exc)
        self._cursor.close()


class PostgresConnection(DatabaseConnection):
    def __init__(self, conn: psycopg.Connection) -> None:
        self._conn = conn
        self._messages: deque[ServerMessage] = deque(maxlen=1000)
        self._custom_types: dict[int, str] = {int(oid): name for oid, name in conn.execute(_CUSTOM_TYPES_SQL)}
        conn.add_notice_handler(self._on_notice)

    def _on_notice(self, diag: psycopg.errors.Diagnostic) -> None:
        severity = MessageSeverity.WARNING if diag.severity_nonlocalized == "WARNING" else MessageSeverity.INFO
        self._messages.append(ServerMessage(severity=severity, text=diag.message_primary or ""))

    def drain_messages(self) -> list[ServerMessage]:
        messages = list(self._messages)
        self._messages.clear()
        return messages

    def _type_name(self, oid: int, display: str | None = None) -> str:
        if oid in self._custom_types:
            return self._custom_types[oid]
        if display and not display.isdigit():
            return display
        info = postgres.types.get(oid)
        return info.name if info else f"oid {oid}"

    def column_meta(self, description: list[psycopg.Column]) -> list[ColumnMeta]:
        return [
            ColumnMeta(
                name=col.name,
                type_name=self._type_name(col.type_code, getattr(col, "type_display", None)),
                category=category_for_oid(col.type_code),
            )
            for col in description
        ]

    def describe(self, sql: str) -> list[ColumnMeta] | None:
        """Column metadata of a statement without executing it (used for zero-row results)."""
        try:
            pgconn = self._conn.pgconn
            encoding = self._conn.info.encoding
            prepared = pgconn.prepare(b"", sql.encode(encoding))
            if prepared.status != pq.ExecStatus.COMMAND_OK:
                return None
            desc = pgconn.describe_prepared(b"")
            if desc.status != pq.ExecStatus.COMMAND_OK or desc.nfields == 0:
                return None
            return [
                ColumnMeta(
                    name=desc.fname(i).decode(encoding),
                    type_name=self._type_name(desc.ftype(i)),
                    category=category_for_oid(desc.ftype(i)),
                )
                for i in range(desc.nfields)
            ]
        except Exception as exc:  # describing is best-effort
            logger.debug("Could not describe statement: {}", exc)
            return None

    def server_info(self) -> ServerInfo:
        version, database, user = self._conn.execute(
            "SELECT current_setting('server_version'), current_database(), current_user"
        ).fetchone()
        return ServerInfo(engine=Engine.POSTGRES, version=f"PostgreSQL {version}", current_database=database, current_user=user)

    def current_database(self) -> str | None:
        return self._conn.info.dbname

    def list_children(self, path: list[PathSegment]) -> list[ObjectNode]:
        raise NotImplementedError  # Task 8

    def object_detail(self, path: list[PathSegment]) -> ObjectDetail:
        raise NotImplementedError  # Task 8

    def run(self, sql: str) -> StatementResult:
        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
        configure_user_loaders(cursor.adapters)
        if _likely_returns_rows(sql):
            return _StreamResult(self, cursor, sql)
        try:
            cursor.execute(sql)
        except psycopg.Error as exc:
            cursor.close()
            raise translate_error(exc, sql) from exc
        return _BufferedResult(self, cursor, sql)

    def in_transaction(self) -> bool:
        return self._conn.info.transaction_status in (pq.TransactionStatus.INTRANS, pq.TransactionStatus.INERROR)

    def cancel(self) -> None:
        try:
            self._conn.cancel_safe(timeout=3.0)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("PostgreSQL cancel failed: {}", exc)

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._conn.close()


class PostgresAdapter(DatabaseAdapter):
    engine = Engine.POSTGRES
    url_schemes = ("postgres", "postgresql")
    default_port = 5432
    capabilities = AdapterCapabilities(per_database_connection=True)

    def parse_url(self, url: str) -> ParsedUrl:
        parsed = urlparse(url)
        if not parsed.hostname:
            raise ValidationFailed("A PostgreSQL URL needs a host, e.g. postgres://user@host:5432/db")
        query = dict(parse_qsl(parsed.query))
        ssl_fields = {_SSL_QUERY_KEYS[k]: query.pop(k) for k in list(query) if k in _SSL_QUERY_KEYS}
        database = unquote(parsed.path.lstrip("/")) or None
        draft = ServerProfileDraft(
            engine="postgres",
            name=f"{parsed.hostname}/{database}" if database else parsed.hostname,
            host=parsed.hostname,
            port=parsed.port,
            database=database,
            username=unquote(parsed.username) if parsed.username else None,
            ssl=SslConfig(**ssl_fields),
            options=query,
        )
        return ParsedUrl(profile=draft, password=unquote(parsed.password) if parsed.password else None)

    def open(
        self, profile: AnyProfile, endpoint: Endpoint | None, password: str | None, database: str | None
    ) -> DatabaseConnection:
        if not isinstance(profile, ServerProfile) or endpoint is None:
            raise ValidationFailed("Not a PostgreSQL server profile")
        params: dict[str, Any] = {
            "host": endpoint.tls_server_name or endpoint.host,
            "port": endpoint.port,
            "dbname": database or profile.database or "postgres",
            "user": profile.username,
            "password": password,
            "connect_timeout": 10,
            "application_name": "DbaGuy",
            "keepalives": 1,
            "keepalives_idle": 30,
            "keepalives_interval": 10,
            "keepalives_count": 3,
            **_ssl_params(profile.ssl),
            **profile.options,
        }
        if endpoint.tls_server_name:
            params["hostaddr"] = endpoint.host  # tunnelled: connect locally, verify TLS against the real host
        params = {k: v for k, v in params.items() if v not in (None, "")}
        try:
            conn = psycopg.connect(autocommit=True, **params)
        except psycopg.Error as exc:
            raise ConnectionFailed(str(exc).strip()) from exc
        logger.info("Connected to PostgreSQL {}:{} db={}", params["host"], endpoint.port, params["dbname"])
        return PostgresConnection(conn)
```

### Task 8: PostgreSQL object tree and DDL

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/introspection.py`, `backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/ddl.py`
- Modify: `backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/adapter.py` (`list_children` / `object_detail` delegate to the new modules and validate the database segment)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_postgres_introspection.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PostgresConnection` (Task 7), `qualified_pg`, `quote_ident_pg`, `quote_literal`, `make_node`, `NodeKind`, `Folder`, `ColumnInfo`, `ObjectDetail` (Task 4), `NotFound`, `ValidationFailed` (Task 1).
- Produces: PG paths
  - `[]` → database nodes
  - `[database]` → schema nodes (user schemas first; `system=True` for `pg_catalog`, `information_schema`, `pg_*`)
  - `[database, schema]` → non-empty folders in order `tables, views, materialized_views, functions, procedures, sequences, types`
  - `[database, schema, folder]` → objects (functions/procedures named `name(argtypes)`; tables have `detail` `"partitioned"` or `"~N rows"`)
  - `[database, schema, folder, object]` → `columns` (+`indexes`, `triggers`, `partitions` when non-empty)
  - `[database, schema, folder, object, folder]` → column/index/trigger/partition leaves
  - `object_detail` for table, partition, view, materialized_view, function, procedure, sequence, type, index, trigger, column, schema.
- `ddl.py` exports `object_detail(conn, path) -> ObjectDetail`; `introspection.py` exports `list_children(conn, path) -> list[ObjectNode]` and `table_columns(conn, qname) -> list[PgColumn]` where `PgColumn(name, type, not_null, default, identity, generated, comment, is_pk)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`backend/tests/test_postgres_introspection.py`:
```python
import pytest

from dbaguy_backend.adapters.base import Endpoint
from dbaguy_backend.adapters.postgres.adapter import PostgresAdapter
from dbaguy_backend.domain.metadata import PathSegment
from tests.support import PG_HOST, PG_PASSWORD, PG_PORT, make_pg_profile, requires_pg

pytestmark = requires_pg


def seg(kind, name):
    return PathSegment(kind=kind, name=name)


DB = [seg("database", "dbaguy")]


def schema_path(schema, folder=None, *rest):
    path = [*DB, seg("schema", schema)]
    if folder:
        path.append(seg("folder", folder))
    return [*path, *rest]


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def conn():
    connection = PostgresAdapter().open(make_pg_profile(), Endpoint(PG_HOST, PG_PORT), PG_PASSWORD, None)
    yield connection
    connection.close()


def names(nodes):
    return [n.name for n in nodes]


def test_databases_and_schemas(conn):
    assert {"dbaguy", "analytics"} <= set(names(conn.list_children([])))
    schemas = conn.list_children(DB)
    assert names(schemas)[:2] == ["public", "sales"]
    system = {n.name for n in schemas if n.system}
    assert "pg_catalog" in system and "public" not in system


def test_folders_hide_empty(conn):
    assert names(conn.list_children(schema_path("public"))) == ["tables", "functions", "sequences", "types"]
    assert names(conn.list_children(schema_path("sales"))) == [
        "tables", "views", "materialized_views", "functions", "procedures", "sequences",
    ]


def test_tables_partitions_and_quoting(conn):
    tables = conn.list_children(schema_path("sales", "tables"))
    assert names(tables) == ["events", "orders"]
    assert tables[0].detail == "partitioned"
    assert "partitions" in names(conn.list_children(tables[0].path))
    [part] = conn.list_children([*tables[0].path, seg("folder", "partitions")])
    assert part.name == "events_2026" and "FOR VALUES" in part.detail
    assert "Order Items" in names(conn.list_children(schema_path("public", "tables")))
    detail = conn.object_detail(schema_path("public", "tables", seg("table", "Order Items")))
    assert 'CREATE TABLE public."Order Items" (' in detail.ddl
    assert '"Select" text' in detail.ddl and '"order" integer' in detail.ddl


def test_columns(conn):
    table = schema_path("public", "tables", seg("table", "customers"))
    cols = conn.list_children([*table, seg("folder", "columns")])
    assert cols[0].name == "id" and "PK" in cols[0].detail
    assert next(c for c in cols if c.name == "email").detail.startswith("email NOT NULL")
    detail = conn.object_detail(table)
    assert detail.comment == "Customer master data"
    assert next(c for c in detail.columns if c.name == "email").comment == "Login e-mail"


def test_table_ddl(conn):
    detail = conn.object_detail(schema_path("sales", "tables", seg("table", "orders")))
    assert detail.ddl_is_generated
    ddl = detail.ddl
    assert "CREATE TABLE sales.orders (" in ddl
    assert "id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY" in ddl
    assert "FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES" in ddl
    assert "CREATE INDEX orders_customer_idx ON sales.orders" in ddl
    events = conn.object_detail(schema_path("sales", "tables", seg("table", "events"))).ddl
    assert "PARTITION BY RANGE (occurred_at)" in events


def test_function_overloads(conn):
    fns = conn.list_children(schema_path("public", "functions"))
    assert names(fns) == ["add_numbers(integer, integer)", "add_numbers(numeric, numeric)"]
    ddl = conn.object_detail(fns[1].path).ddl
    assert "numeric" in ddl and ddl.rstrip().endswith(";")
    [proc] = conn.list_children(schema_path("sales", "procedures"))
    assert "CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE sales.archive_orders" in conn.object_detail(proc.path).ddl


def test_other_object_ddl(conn):
    def ddl(*path):
        return conn.object_detail(list(path)).ddl

    assert "AS ENUM ('pending', 'paid', 'shipped', 'cancelled')" in ddl(*schema_path("public", "types", seg("type", "order_status")))
    domain = ddl(*schema_path("public", "types", seg("type", "email")))
    assert domain.startswith("CREATE DOMAIN public.email AS text") and "CHECK" in domain
    composite = ddl(*schema_path("public", "types", seg("type", "address")))
    assert composite.startswith("CREATE TYPE public.address AS (") and "street text" in composite
    seq = ddl(*schema_path("public", "sequences", seg("sequence", "invoice_seq")))
    assert "INCREMENT BY 10" in seq and "START WITH 1000" in seq
    assert "CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW sales.order_summary AS" in ddl(*schema_path("sales", "views", seg("view", "order_summary")))
    assert "CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW sales.daily_totals AS" in ddl(
        *schema_path("sales", "materialized_views", seg("materialized_view", "daily_totals"))
    )
    trigger = ddl(*schema_path("sales", "tables", seg("table", "orders"), seg("folder", "triggers"), seg("trigger", "orders_noop")))
    assert "EXECUTE FUNCTION sales.orders_noop()" in trigger
    index = ddl(*schema_path("sales", "tables", seg("table", "orders"), seg("folder", "indexes"), seg("index", "orders_customer_idx")))
    assert index.startswith("CREATE INDEX orders_customer_idx")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement introspection**

`backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/introspection.py`:
```python
from dataclasses import dataclass

import psycopg

from ...domain.errors import NotFound
from ...domain.metadata import ColumnInfo, Folder, NodeKind, ObjectNode, PathSegment, make_node
from ..sql_text import qualified_pg

SCHEMA_OID = "(SELECT oid FROM pg_namespace WHERE nspname = %(schema)s)"

_SCHEMAS_SQL = """
SELECT n.nspname,
       (n.nspname IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema') OR n.nspname LIKE 'pg\\_%%') AS is_system
FROM pg_namespace n
WHERE n.nspname NOT LIKE 'pg\\_temp\\_%%' AND n.nspname NOT LIKE 'pg\\_toast%%'
ORDER BY is_system, n.nspname
"""

_FOLDER_COUNTS_SQL = """
SELECT
  (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_class c WHERE c.relnamespace = n.oid AND c.relkind IN ('r', 'p') AND NOT c.relispartition),
  (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_class c WHERE c.relnamespace = n.oid AND c.relkind = 'v'),
  (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_class c WHERE c.relnamespace = n.oid AND c.relkind = 'm'),
  (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_proc p WHERE p.pronamespace = n.oid AND p.prokind IN ('f', 'w')),
  (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_proc p WHERE p.pronamespace = n.oid AND p.prokind = 'p'),
  (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_class c WHERE c.relnamespace = n.oid AND c.relkind = 'S'),
  (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_type t LEFT JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = t.typrelid
    WHERE t.typnamespace = n.oid AND (t.typtype IN ('e', 'd') OR (t.typtype = 'c' AND c.relkind = 'c')))
FROM pg_namespace n WHERE n.nspname = %(schema)s
"""
_FOLDER_ORDER = (
    Folder.TABLES, Folder.VIEWS, Folder.MATERIALIZED_VIEWS, Folder.FUNCTIONS,
    Folder.PROCEDURES, Folder.SEQUENCES, Folder.TYPES,
)

_RELKIND_BY_FOLDER = {Folder.VIEWS: ("v", NodeKind.VIEW), Folder.MATERIALIZED_VIEWS: ("m", NodeKind.MATERIALIZED_VIEW),
                      Folder.SEQUENCES: ("S", NodeKind.SEQUENCE)}
_TYPE_LABEL = {"e": "enum", "d": "domain", "c": "composite"}

_COLUMNS_SQL = """
SELECT a.attname, format_type(a.atttypid, a.atttypmod), a.attnotnull, pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid),
       a.attidentity::text, a.attgenerated::text, col_description(a.attrelid, a.attnum),
       EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_index i WHERE i.indrelid = a.attrelid AND i.indisprimary AND a.attnum = ANY (i.indkey))
FROM pg_attribute a
LEFT JOIN pg_attrdef d ON d.adrelid = a.attrelid AND d.adnum = a.attnum
WHERE a.attrelid = %(oid)s AND a.attnum > 0 AND NOT a.attisdropped
ORDER BY a.attnum
"""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PgColumn:
    name: str
    type: str
    not_null: bool
    default: str | None
    identity: str  # '' | 'a' (ALWAYS) | 'd' (BY DEFAULT)
    generated: str  # '' | 's' (stored) | 'v' (virtual, PG 18)
    comment: str | None
    is_pk: bool

    def info(self) -> ColumnInfo:
        return ColumnInfo(
            name=self.name, type_name=self.type, nullable=not self.not_null,
            default=self.default, is_primary_key=self.is_pk, comment=self.comment,
        )

    def detail(self) -> str:
        parts = [self.type]
        if self.not_null:
            parts.append("NOT NULL")
        if self.is_pk:
            parts.append("PK")
        return " ".join(parts)


def relation_oid(conn: psycopg.Connection, qname: str) -> int:
    row = conn.execute("SELECT to_regclass(%s)::oid", (qname,)).fetchone()
    if row is None or row[0] is None:
        raise NotFound(f"Relation {qname} not found")
    return int(row[0])


def table_columns(conn: psycopg.Connection, oid: int) -> list[PgColumn]:
    return [PgColumn(*row) for row in conn.execute(_COLUMNS_SQL, {"oid": oid})]


def list_children(conn: psycopg.Connection, path: list[PathSegment]) -> list[ObjectNode]:
    if not path:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT datname FROM pg_database WHERE datallowconn AND NOT datistemplate ORDER BY datname"
        )
        return [make_node(path, NodeKind.DATABASE, name, has_children=True) for (name,) in rows]
    rel = path[1:]  # path[0] is the database this connection is bound to
    if not rel:
        return [
            make_node(path, NodeKind.SCHEMA, name, has_children=True, system=is_system)
            for name, is_system in conn.execute(_SCHEMAS_SQL)
        ]
    schema = rel[0].name
    if len(rel) == 1:
        row = conn.execute(_FOLDER_COUNTS_SQL, {"schema": schema}).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            raise NotFound(f"Schema {schema} not found")
        return [make_node(path, NodeKind.FOLDER, f, has_children=True) for f, n in zip(_FOLDER_ORDER, row) if n]
    if len(rel) == 2:
        return _schema_objects(conn, path, schema, Folder(rel[1].name))
    if len(rel) == 3:
        return _object_folders(conn, path, schema, rel[2])
    if len(rel) == 4:
        return _object_children(conn, path, schema, rel[2], Folder(rel[3].name))
    return []


def _schema_objects(conn: psycopg.Connection, path: list[PathSegment], schema: str, folder: Folder) -> list[ObjectNode]:
    params = {"schema": schema}
    if folder == Folder.TABLES:
        rows = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT c.relname, c.relkind::text, c.reltuples::float8 FROM pg_class c "
            f"WHERE c.relnamespace = {SCHEMA_OID} AND c.relkind IN ('r', 'p') AND NOT c.relispartition ORDER BY c.relname",
            params,
        )
        return [
            make_node(
                path, NodeKind.TABLE, name, has_children=True,
                detail="partitioned" if relkind == "p" else (f"~{int(est):,} rows" if est >= 0 else None),
            )
            for name, relkind, est in rows
        ]
    if folder in _RELKIND_BY_FOLDER:
        relkind, kind = _RELKIND_BY_FOLDER[folder]
        rows = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT c.relname FROM pg_class c WHERE c.relnamespace = {SCHEMA_OID} AND c.relkind = %(relkind)s "
            f"ORDER BY c.relname",
            {**params, "relkind": relkind},
        )
        return [make_node(path, kind, name, has_children=kind != NodeKind.SEQUENCE) for (name,) in rows]
    if folder in (Folder.FUNCTIONS, Folder.PROCEDURES):
        kinds = ["f", "w"] if folder == Folder.FUNCTIONS else ["p"]
        kind = NodeKind.FUNCTION if folder == Folder.FUNCTIONS else NodeKind.PROCEDURE
        rows = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT p.proname || '(' || oidvectortypes(p.proargtypes) || ')', pg_get_function_result(p.oid) "
            f"FROM pg_proc p WHERE p.pronamespace = {SCHEMA_OID} AND p.prokind::text = ANY(%(kinds)s) ORDER BY 1",
            {**params, "kinds": kinds},
        )
        return [make_node(path, kind, sig, has_children=False, detail=result) for sig, result in rows]
    if folder == Folder.TYPES:
        rows = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT t.typname, t.typtype::text FROM pg_type t LEFT JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = t.typrelid "
            f"WHERE t.typnamespace = {SCHEMA_OID} AND (t.typtype IN ('e', 'd') OR (t.typtype = 'c' AND c.relkind = 'c')) "
            f"ORDER BY t.typname",
            params,
        )
        return [make_node(path, NodeKind.TYPE, name, has_children=False, detail=_TYPE_LABEL.get(t)) for name, t in rows]
    return []


def _object_folders(conn: psycopg.Connection, path: list[PathSegment], schema: str, obj: PathSegment) -> list[ObjectNode]:
    if obj.kind not in (NodeKind.TABLE, NodeKind.VIEW, NodeKind.MATERIALIZED_VIEW):
        return []
    oid = relation_oid(conn, qualified_pg(schema, obj.name))
    indexes, triggers, partitions = conn.execute(
        "SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_index WHERE indrelid = %(oid)s), "
        "(SELECT count(*) FROM pg_trigger WHERE tgrelid = %(oid)s AND NOT tgisinternal), "
        "(SELECT count(*) FROM pg_inherits WHERE inhparent = %(oid)s)",
        {"oid": oid},
    ).fetchone()
    folders = [Folder.COLUMNS]
    if indexes:
        folders.append(Folder.INDEXES)
    if triggers:
        folders.append(Folder.TRIGGERS)
    if partitions and obj.kind == NodeKind.TABLE:
        folders.append(Folder.PARTITIONS)
    return [make_node(path, NodeKind.FOLDER, f, has_children=True) for f in folders]


def _object_children(
    conn: psycopg.Connection, path: list[PathSegment], schema: str, obj: PathSegment, folder: Folder
) -> list[ObjectNode]:
    oid = relation_oid(conn, qualified_pg(schema, obj.name))
    if folder == Folder.COLUMNS:
        return [make_node(path, NodeKind.COLUMN, c.name, has_children=False, detail=c.detail()) for c in table_columns(conn, oid)]
    if folder == Folder.INDEXES:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT ic.relname, i.indisprimary, i.indisunique FROM pg_index i JOIN pg_class ic ON ic.oid = i.indexrelid "
            "WHERE i.indrelid = %(oid)s ORDER BY ic.relname",
            {"oid": oid},
        )
        return [
            make_node(path, NodeKind.INDEX, name, has_children=False,
                      detail="primary" if primary else ("unique" if unique else None))
            for name, primary, unique in rows
        ]
    if folder == Folder.TRIGGERS:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT tgname FROM pg_trigger WHERE tgrelid = %(oid)s AND NOT tgisinternal ORDER BY tgname", {"oid": oid}
        )
        return [make_node(path, NodeKind.TRIGGER, name, has_children=False) for (name,) in rows]
    if folder == Folder.PARTITIONS:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT c.relname, pg_get_expr(c.relpartbound, c.oid) FROM pg_inherits h JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = h.inhrelid "
            "WHERE h.inhparent = %(oid)s ORDER BY c.relname",
            {"oid": oid},
        )
        return [make_node(path, NodeKind.PARTITION, name, has_children=False, detail=bound) for name, bound in rows]
    return []
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement DDL**

`backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/ddl.py`:
```python
import psycopg

from ...domain.errors import NotFound, ValidationFailed
from ...domain.metadata import NodeKind, ObjectDetail, PathSegment
from ..sql_text import qualified_pg, quote_ident_pg, quote_literal
from .introspection import PgColumn, relation_oid, table_columns

_CONSTRAINTS_SQL = """
SELECT conname, pg_get_constraintdef(oid, true) FROM pg_constraint
WHERE conrelid = %(oid)s AND contype IN ('p', 'u', 'c', 'f', 'x')
ORDER BY CASE contype WHEN 'p' THEN 0 WHEN 'u' THEN 1 WHEN 'c' THEN 2 WHEN 'f' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END, conname
"""
_EXTRA_INDEXES_SQL = """
SELECT pg_get_indexdef(i.indexrelid) FROM pg_index i
WHERE i.indrelid = %(oid)s
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint k WHERE k.conindid = i.indexrelid AND k.conrelid = i.indrelid)
ORDER BY 1
"""


def _one(conn: psycopg.Connection, sql: str, params: dict, what: str):
    row = conn.execute(sql, params).fetchone()
    if row is None or row[0] is None:
        raise NotFound(f"{what} not found")
    return row


def _column_def(col: PgColumn) -> str:
    parts = [quote_ident_pg(col.name), col.type]
    if col.generated in ("s", "v"):
        parts.append(f"GENERATED ALWAYS AS ({col.default}) {'STORED' if col.generated == 's' else 'VIRTUAL'}")
    elif col.identity == "a":
        parts.append("GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY")
    elif col.identity == "d":
        parts.append("GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY")
    elif col.default is not None:
        parts.append(f"DEFAULT {col.default}")
    if col.not_null and not col.identity:
        parts.append("NOT NULL")
    return " ".join(parts)


def table_ddl(conn: psycopg.Connection, schema: str, name: str) -> tuple[str, list[PgColumn], str | None]:
    qname = qualified_pg(schema, name)
    oid = relation_oid(conn, qname)
    relkind, is_partition, partkey, comment, parent, bound, persistence = conn.execute(
        "SELECT c.relkind::text, c.relispartition, pg_get_partkeydef(c.oid), obj_description(c.oid, 'pg_class'), "
        "(SELECT h.inhparent::regclass::text FROM pg_inherits h WHERE h.inhrelid = c.oid LIMIT 1), "
        "pg_get_expr(c.relpartbound, c.oid), c.relpersistence::text FROM pg_class c WHERE c.oid = %(oid)s",
        {"oid": oid},
    ).fetchone()
    columns = table_columns(conn, oid)
    blocks: list[str] = []
    if is_partition:
        blocks.append(f"CREATE TABLE {qname} PARTITION OF {parent}\n    {bound};")
    else:
        body = [_column_def(c) for c in columns]
        body += [f"CONSTRAINT {quote_ident_pg(n)} {d}" for n, d in conn.execute(_CONSTRAINTS_SQL, {"oid": oid})]
        unlogged = "UNLOGGED " if persistence == "u" else ""
        statement = f"CREATE {unlogged}TABLE {qname} (\n    " + ",\n    ".join(body) + "\n)"
        if relkind == "p":
            statement += f" PARTITION BY {partkey}"
        blocks.append(statement + ";")
    indexes = [f"{d};" for (d,) in conn.execute(_EXTRA_INDEXES_SQL, {"oid": oid})]
    if indexes:
        blocks.append("\n".join(indexes))
    comments = []
    if comment:
        comments.append(f"COMMENT ON TABLE {qname} IS {quote_literal(comment)};")
    comments += [
        f"COMMENT ON COLUMN {qname}.{quote_ident_pg(c.name)} IS {quote_literal(c.comment)};" for c in columns if c.comment
    ]
    if comments:
        blocks.append("\n".join(comments))
    return "\n\n".join(blocks), columns, comment


def _view_ddl(conn: psycopg.Connection, qname: str, materialized: bool) -> str:
    (definition,) = _one(conn, "SELECT pg_get_viewdef(to_regclass(%(q)s), true)", {"q": qname}, qname)
    keyword = "CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW" if materialized else "CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW"
    return f"{keyword} {qname} AS\n{definition.rstrip().rstrip(';')};"


def _function_ddl(conn: psycopg.Connection, schema: str, signature: str) -> str:
    name, _, args = signature.partition("(")
    regproc = f"{qualified_pg(schema, name)}({args}"
    (definition,) = _one(
        conn, "SELECT pg_get_functiondef(p) FROM to_regprocedure(%(sig)s) p", {"sig": regproc}, signature
    )
    return definition.rstrip() + ";"


def _sequence_ddl(conn: psycopg.Connection, qname: str) -> str:
    type_, start, inc, minv, maxv, cache, cycle = _one(
        conn,
        "SELECT format_type(s.seqtypid, NULL), s.seqstart, s.seqincrement, s.seqmin, s.seqmax, s.seqcache, s.seqcycle "
        "FROM pg_sequence s WHERE s.seqrelid = to_regclass(%(q)s)",
        {"q": qname},
        qname,
    )
    return (
        f"CREATE SEQUENCE {qname}\n    AS {type_}\n    INCREMENT BY {inc}\n    MINVALUE {minv}\n"
        f"    MAXVALUE {maxv}\n    START WITH {start}\n    CACHE {cache}\n    {'CYCLE' if cycle else 'NO CYCLE'};"
    )


def _type_ddl(conn: psycopg.Connection, qname: str) -> str:
    oid, typtype, base, not_null, default, typrelid = _one(
        conn,
        "SELECT t.oid, t.typtype::text, format_type(t.typbasetype, t.typtypmod), t.typnotnull, t.typdefault, t.typrelid "
        "FROM pg_type t WHERE t.oid = to_regtype(%(q)s)",
        {"q": qname},
        qname,
    )
    if typtype == "e":
        labels = [quote_literal(label) for (label,) in conn.execute(
            "SELECT enumlabel FROM pg_enum WHERE enumtypid = %(oid)s ORDER BY enumsortorder", {"oid": oid})]
        return f"CREATE TYPE {qname} AS ENUM ({', '.join(labels)});"
    if typtype == "d":
        statement = f"CREATE DOMAIN {qname} AS {base}"
        if default is not None:
            statement += f" DEFAULT {default}"
        if not_null:
            statement += " NOT NULL"
        for name, definition in conn.execute(
            "SELECT conname, pg_get_constraintdef(oid, true) FROM pg_constraint WHERE contypid = %(oid)s ORDER BY conname",
            {"oid": oid},
        ):
            statement += f"\n    CONSTRAINT {quote_ident_pg(name)} {definition}"
        return statement + ";"
    if typtype == "c":
        attributes = [f"{quote_ident_pg(n)} {t}" for n, t in conn.execute(
            "SELECT attname, format_type(atttypid, atttypmod) FROM pg_attribute "
            "WHERE attrelid = %(rel)s AND attnum > 0 AND NOT attisdropped ORDER BY attnum",
            {"rel": typrelid},
        )]
        return f"CREATE TYPE {qname} AS (\n    " + ",\n    ".join(attributes) + "\n);"
    raise ValidationFailed(f"No DDL available for type kind '{typtype}'")


def object_detail(conn: psycopg.Connection, path: list[PathSegment]) -> ObjectDetail:
    rel = path[1:]
    if not rel:
        raise ValidationFailed("No details available for a database")
    last = path[-1]
    schema = rel[0].name
    base = {"path": path, "kind": last.kind, "name": last.name}
    if last.kind == NodeKind.SCHEMA:
        return ObjectDetail(**base, ddl=f"CREATE SCHEMA {quote_ident_pg(schema)};")
    qname = qualified_pg(schema, last.name)
    match last.kind:
        case NodeKind.TABLE | NodeKind.PARTITION:
            ddl, columns, comment = table_ddl(conn, schema, last.name)
            return ObjectDetail(**base, ddl=ddl, ddl_is_generated=True, columns=[c.info() for c in columns], comment=comment)
        case NodeKind.VIEW | NodeKind.MATERIALIZED_VIEW:
            columns = [c.info() for c in table_columns(conn, relation_oid(conn, qname))]
            return ObjectDetail(**base, ddl=_view_ddl(conn, qname, last.kind == NodeKind.MATERIALIZED_VIEW), columns=columns)
        case NodeKind.FUNCTION | NodeKind.PROCEDURE:
            return ObjectDetail(**base, ddl=_function_ddl(conn, schema, last.name))
        case NodeKind.SEQUENCE:
            return ObjectDetail(**base, ddl=_sequence_ddl(conn, qname))
        case NodeKind.TYPE:
            return ObjectDetail(**base, ddl=_type_ddl(conn, qname))
        case NodeKind.INDEX:
            (definition,) = _one(conn, "SELECT pg_get_indexdef(to_regclass(%(q)s))", {"q": qname}, qname)
            return ObjectDetail(**base, ddl=f"{definition};")
        case NodeKind.TRIGGER:
            table = qualified_pg(schema, rel[2].name)
            (definition,) = _one(
                conn,
                "SELECT pg_get_triggerdef(t.oid, true) FROM pg_trigger t WHERE t.tgrelid = to_regclass(%(t)s) AND t.tgname = %(n)s",
                {"t": table, "n": last.name},
                last.name,
            )
            return ObjectDetail(**base, ddl=f"{definition};")
        case NodeKind.COLUMN:
            table_oid = relation_oid(conn, qualified_pg(schema, rel[2].name))
            columns = [c.info() for c in table_columns(conn, table_oid) if c.name == last.name]
            if not columns:
                raise NotFound(f"Column {last.name} not found")
            return ObjectDetail(**base, columns=columns)
    raise ValidationFailed(f"No details available for {last.kind}")
```

Modify `backend/dbaguy_backend/adapters/postgres/adapter.py`: add imports `from . import ddl, introspection` and replace the two `NotImplementedError` methods with:
```python
    def _check_database(self, path: list[PathSegment]) -> None:
        if path and path[0].name != self.current_database():
            raise ValidationFailed(f"Connection is bound to database '{self.current_database()}', not '{path[0].name}'")

    def list_children(self, path: list[PathSegment]) -> list[ObjectNode]:
        self._check_database(path)
        return introspection.list_children(self._conn, path)

    def object_detail(self, path: list[PathSegment]) -> ObjectDetail:
        self._check_database(path)
        return ddl.object_detail(self._conn, path)
```

  - `ConnectionLease(connection, release)` with `close()`.
  - `ConnectionService(registry, profiles, tunnels)` with `open(profile, database=None, secrets=None) -> ConnectionLease` and `test(request: ConnectionTestRequest) -> ConnectionTestResult`.

`backend/tests/test_ssh_tunnel.py`:
```python
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from dbaguy_backend.adapters.postgres.adapter import PostgresAdapter
from dbaguy_backend.connectivity.ssh_tunnel import SshTunnelManager, write_askpass_script
from dbaguy_backend.domain.errors import TunnelFailed
from dbaguy_backend.domain.profiles import SshConfig
from tests.support import BASTION_PORT, PG_PASSWORD, make_pg_profile, requires_bastion

TEST_SSH_OPTIONS = ["-o", "UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null", "-o", "LogLevel=ERROR"]


def manager(tmp_path) -> SshTunnelManager:
    return SshTunnelManager(write_askpass_script(tmp_path), extra_options=TEST_SSH_OPTIONS)


def bastion(**overrides) -> SshConfig:
    return SshConfig(**{"enabled": True, "host": "127.0.0.1", "port": BASTION_PORT, "user": "dev", "auth": "password", **overrides})


def test_password_required(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(TunnelFailed, match="SSH password is required"):
        manager(tmp_path).acquire(uuid4(), bastion(), "postgres", 5432, None)


@requires_bastion
def test_password_tunnel_to_postgres(tmp_path):
    tunnels = manager(tmp_path)
    pid = uuid4()
    endpoint = tunnels.acquire(pid, bastion(), "postgres", 5432, "dev")
    try:
        assert endpoint.host == "127.0.0.1" and endpoint.tls_server_name == "postgres"
        conn = PostgresAdapter().open(make_pg_profile(host="postgres", port=5432), endpoint, PG_PASSWORD, None)
        assert conn.run("SELECT 1").fetch(1) == [[1]]
        conn.close()
        assert tunnels.acquire(pid, bastion(), "postgres", 5432, "dev").port == endpoint.port  # shared, ref-counted
        tunnels.release(pid, "postgres", 5432)
        assert tunnels.active_count() == 1
    finally:
        tunnels.release(pid, "postgres", 5432)
    assert tunnels.active_count() == 0


@requires_bastion
def test_wrong_password_fails_with_ssh_error(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(TunnelFailed, match="(?i)permission denied"):
        manager(tmp_path).acquire(uuid4(), bastion(), "postgres", 5432, "wrong")
```

```python
import sqlite3

from dbaguy_backend.adapters.postgres.adapter import PostgresAdapter
from dbaguy_backend.adapters.registry import AdapterRegistry
from dbaguy_backend.adapters.sqlite.adapter import SqliteAdapter
from dbaguy_backend.connectivity.ssh_tunnel import SshTunnelManager, write_askpass_script
from dbaguy_backend.domain.profiles import ConnectionTestRequest, ProfileSecretsInput
from dbaguy_backend.services.connection_service import ConnectionService
from dbaguy_backend.services.profile_service import ProfileService
from dbaguy_backend.storage.paths import profiles_file
from dbaguy_backend.storage.profile_store import ProfileStore
from dbaguy_backend.storage.secret_store import InMemorySecretStore
from tests.support import BASTION_PORT, PG_PASSWORD, PG_PORT, requires_bastion, requires_pg
from tests.test_ssh_tunnel import TEST_SSH_OPTIONS


def make_service(tmp_path) -> ConnectionService:
    profiles = ProfileService(ProfileStore(profiles_file(tmp_path)), InMemorySecretStore())
    tunnels = SshTunnelManager(write_askpass_script(tmp_path), extra_options=TEST_SSH_OPTIONS)
    return ConnectionService(AdapterRegistry([PostgresAdapter(), SqliteAdapter()]), profiles, tunnels)


def test_sqlite_ok_and_missing(tmp_path):
    db = tmp_path / "a.db"
    sqlite3.connect(db).close()
    service = make_service(tmp_path)
    ok = service.test(ConnectionTestRequest.model_validate({"profile": {"engine": "sqlite", "name": "a", "path": str(db)}}))
    assert ok.ok and ok.server_version
    missing = service.test(
        ConnectionTestRequest.model_validate({"profile": {"engine": "sqlite", "name": "b", "path": str(tmp_path / "nope.db")}})
    )
    assert not missing.ok and "not found" in missing.message


@requires_pg
def test_postgres_wrong_password_reports_failure(tmp_path):
    request = ConnectionTestRequest.model_validate({
        "profile": {"engine": "postgres", "name": "pg", "host": "127.0.0.1", "port": PG_PORT,
                    "database": "dbaguy", "username": "dbaguy", "ssl": {"mode": "disable"}},
        "secrets": {"password": "wrong"},
    })
    result = make_service(tmp_path).test(request)
    assert not result.ok and "password authentication failed" in result.message


@requires_bastion
def test_postgres_through_ssh_bastion(tmp_path):
    request = ConnectionTestRequest(
        profile={
            "engine": "postgres", "name": "pg via ssh", "host": "postgres", "port": 5432, "database": "dbaguy",
            "username": "dbaguy", "ssl": {"mode": "disable"},
            "ssh": {"enabled": True, "host": "127.0.0.1", "port": BASTION_PORT, "user": "dev", "auth": "password"},
        },
        secrets=ProfileSecretsInput(password=PG_PASSWORD, ssh_password="dev"),
    )
    service = make_service(tmp_path)
    result = service.test(request)
    assert result.ok, result.message
    assert service._tunnels.active_count() == 0  # released after the test
```

`backend/dbaguy_backend/connectivity/ssh_tunnel.py`:
```python
import os
import socket
import subprocess
import threading
import time
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import UUID

from loguru import logger

from ..adapters.base import Endpoint
from ..domain.errors import TunnelFailed
from ..domain.profiles import SshConfig

_ASKPASS = "#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s\\n' \"$DBAGUY_ASKPASS_SECRET\"\n"


def write_askpass_script(directory: Path) -> Path:
    """ssh calls SSH_ASKPASS for the password; the secret only travels in the child's environment."""
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = directory / "askpass.sh"
    path.write_text(_ASKPASS)
    path.chmod(0o700)
    return path


def _free_port() -> int:
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        return sock.getsockname()[1]


def _port_accepts(port: int) -> bool:
    try:
        socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port), timeout=0.2).close()
        return True
    except OSError:
        return False


@dataclass
class _Tunnel:
    process: subprocess.Popen
    local_port: int
    refcount: int = 1
    stderr_tail: deque = field(default_factory=lambda: deque(maxlen=50))


class SshTunnelManager:
    """Local port forwards through the system `ssh` (honours ~/.ssh/config, agent, ProxyJump)."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        askpass_path: Path,
        ready_timeout: float = 10.0,
        extra_options: list[str] | None = None,
        ssh_binary: str = "ssh",
    ) -> None:
        self._askpass = askpass_path
        self._ready_timeout = ready_timeout
        self._extra = list(extra_options or [])
        self._ssh = ssh_binary
        self._tunnels: dict[tuple[UUID, str, int], _Tunnel] = {}
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def acquire(
        self, profile_id: UUID, ssh: SshConfig, target_host: str, target_port: int, password: str | None
    ) -> Endpoint:
        key = (profile_id, target_host, target_port)
        with self._lock:
            tunnel = self._tunnels.get(key)
            if tunnel is not None and tunnel.process.poll() is None:
                tunnel.refcount += 1
            else:
                tunnel = self._start(ssh, target_host, target_port, password)
                self._tunnels[key] = tunnel
            return Endpoint(host="127.0.0.1", port=tunnel.local_port, tls_server_name=target_host)

    def release(self, profile_id: UUID, target_host: str, target_port: int) -> None:
        key = (profile_id, target_host, target_port)
        with self._lock:
            tunnel = self._tunnels.get(key)
            if tunnel is None:
                return
            tunnel.refcount -= 1
            if tunnel.refcount > 0:
                return
            del self._tunnels[key]
        self._stop(tunnel)
        logger.info("Closed SSH tunnel to {}:{}", target_host, target_port)

    def active_count(self) -> int:
        with self._lock:
            return sum(1 for t in self._tunnels.values() if t.process.poll() is None)

    def close_all(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            tunnels = list(self._tunnels.values())
            self._tunnels.clear()
        for tunnel in tunnels:
            self._stop(tunnel)

    def _command(self, ssh: SshConfig, local_port: int, target_host: str, target_port: int) -> list[str]:
        cmd = [
            self._ssh, *self._extra,  # extra options first: ssh uses the first value it sees
            "-N", "-L", f"127.0.0.1:{local_port}:{target_host}:{target_port}",
            "-o", "ExitOnForwardFailure=yes",
            "-o", "ServerAliveInterval=15",
            "-o", "ServerAliveCountMax=3",
            "-o", "ConnectTimeout=10",
            "-o", "StrictHostKeyChecking=accept-new",
        ]
        if ssh.port:
            cmd += ["-p", str(ssh.port)]
        if ssh.user:
            cmd += ["-l", ssh.user]
        if ssh.identity_file:
            cmd += ["-i", os.path.expanduser(ssh.identity_file), "-o", "IdentitiesOnly=yes"]
        if ssh.auth == "password":
            cmd += [
                "-o", "PreferredAuthentications=password,keyboard-interactive",
                "-o", "PubkeyAuthentication=no",
                "-o", "NumberOfPasswordPrompts=1",
            ]
        else:
            cmd += ["-o", "BatchMode=yes"]
        cmd.append(ssh.host)
        return cmd

    def _start(self, ssh: SshConfig, target_host: str, target_port: int, password: str | None) -> _Tunnel:
        if not ssh.host:
            raise TunnelFailed("SSH host is required")
        env = os.environ.copy()
        if ssh.auth == "password":
            if not password:
                raise TunnelFailed("SSH password is required")
            env.update({
                "SSH_ASKPASS": str(self._askpass),
                "SSH_ASKPASS_REQUIRE": "force",
                "DISPLAY": env.get("DISPLAY", ":0"),
                "DBAGUY_ASKPASS_SECRET": password,
            })
        local_port = _free_port()
        logger.info("Opening SSH tunnel via {} to {}:{} (local port {})", ssh.host, target_host, target_port, local_port)
        process = subprocess.Popen(
            self._command(ssh, local_port, target_host, target_port),
            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            env=env, start_new_session=True, text=True,
        )
        tunnel = _Tunnel(process=process, local_port=local_port)
        threading.Thread(target=self._drain, args=(tunnel,), name="ssh-stderr", daemon=True).start()
        deadline = time.monotonic() + self._ready_timeout
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            if process.poll() is not None:
                time.sleep(0.1)  # let the drain thread read the last lines
                logger.warning("SSH tunnel to {} failed", ssh.host)
                raise TunnelFailed(f"SSH tunnel failed: {self._error_text(tunnel)}")
            if _port_accepts(local_port):
                return tunnel
            time.sleep(0.1)
        self._stop(tunnel)
        raise TunnelFailed(f"SSH tunnel to {ssh.host} timed out: {self._error_text(tunnel)}")

    @staticmethod
    def _drain(tunnel: _Tunnel) -> None:
        assert tunnel.process.stderr is not None
        for line in tunnel.process.stderr:
            line = line.rstrip()
            if line:
                tunnel.stderr_tail.append(line)
                logger.debug("[ssh] {}", line)

    @staticmethod
    def _error_text(tunnel: _Tunnel) -> str:
        return "\n".join(tunnel.stderr_tail) or f"ssh exited with code {tunnel.process.returncode}"

    @staticmethod
    def _stop(tunnel: _Tunnel) -> None:
        if tunnel.process.poll() is None:
            tunnel.process.terminate()
            try:
                tunnel.process.wait(timeout=2)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                tunnel.process.kill()
```

`backend/dbaguy_backend/services/connection_service.py`:
```python
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from uuid import uuid4

from loguru import logger

from ..adapters.base import DatabaseAdapter, DatabaseConnection, Endpoint, StatementError
from ..adapters.registry import AdapterRegistry
from ..connectivity.ssh_tunnel import SshTunnelManager
from ..domain.errors import DomainError, ValidationFailed
from ..domain.profiles import AnyProfile, ConnectionTestRequest, ConnectionTestResult, ServerProfile, profile_from_draft
from .profile_service import ProfileService, ResolvedSecrets


def _noop() -> None:
    return None


@dataclass
class ConnectionLease:
    """An open connection plus whatever it holds (an SSH tunnel)."""

    connection: DatabaseConnection
    release: Callable[[], None] = field(default=_noop)

    def close(self) -> None:
        try:
            self.connection.close()
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Error while closing connection: {}", exc)
        finally:
            self.release()


class ConnectionService:
    def __init__(self, registry: AdapterRegistry, profiles: ProfileService, tunnels: SshTunnelManager) -> None:
        self._registry = registry
        self._profiles = profiles
        self._tunnels = tunnels

    def open(
        self, profile: AnyProfile, database: str | None = None, secrets: ResolvedSecrets | None = None
    ) -> ConnectionLease:
        secrets = secrets if secrets is not None else self._profiles.resolve_secrets(profile)
        adapter = self._registry.get(profile.engine)
        endpoint, release = self._endpoint(adapter, profile, secrets)
        try:
            connection = adapter.open(profile, endpoint, secrets.password, database)
        except BaseException:
            release()
            raise
        return ConnectionLease(connection, release)

    def _endpoint(
        self, adapter: DatabaseAdapter, profile: AnyProfile, secrets: ResolvedSecrets
    ) -> tuple[Endpoint | None, Callable[[], None]]:
        if not isinstance(profile, ServerProfile):
            return None, _noop
        port = profile.port or adapter.default_port
        if port is None:
            raise ValidationFailed("A port is required")
        if not profile.ssh.enabled:
            return Endpoint(profile.host, port), _noop
        endpoint = self._tunnels.acquire(profile.id, profile.ssh, profile.host, port, secrets.ssh_password)
        return endpoint, lambda: self._tunnels.release(profile.id, profile.host, port)

    def test(self, request: ConnectionTestRequest) -> ConnectionTestResult:
        started = time.monotonic()

        def elapsed() -> int:
            return int((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000)

        profile = profile_from_draft(request.profile, request.existing_id or uuid4())
        try:
            lease = self.open(profile, secrets=self._test_secrets(request))
            try:
                info = lease.connection.server_info()
            finally:
                lease.close()
        except (DomainError, StatementError) as exc:
            logger.info("Connection test for '{}' failed: {}", profile.name, exc.message)
            return ConnectionTestResult(ok=False, message=exc.message, elapsed_ms=elapsed())
        return ConnectionTestResult(
            ok=True, message=f"Connected to {info.version}", server_version=info.version, elapsed_ms=elapsed()
        )

    def _test_secrets(self, request: ConnectionTestRequest) -> ResolvedSecrets:
        stored = ResolvedSecrets()
        if request.existing_id is not None:
            try:
                stored = self._profiles.resolve_secrets(self._profiles.get(request.existing_id))
            except DomainError:
                stored = ResolvedSecrets()
        given = request.secrets
        return ResolvedSecrets(
            password=given.password if given.password is not None else stored.password,
            ssh_password=given.ssh_password if given.ssh_password is not None else stored.ssh_password,
        )
```

### Task 10: Session worker, session manager, metadata service

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/dbaguy_backend/sessions/__init__.py`, `backend/dbaguy_backend/sessions/worker.py`, `backend/dbaguy_backend/sessions/manager.py`, `backend/dbaguy_backend/services/metadata_service.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_sessions.py`, `backend/tests/test_metadata_service.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ConnectionService`, `ConnectionLease` (Task 9); `ProfileService` (Task 3); `AdapterRegistry`, `DatabaseConnection`, `StatementError`, `StatementCancelled`, `StatementResult` (Task 4); all execution models (Task 4); `MetadataRequest`, `ObjectNode`, `ObjectDetail`, `NodeKind`, `ServerInfo` (Task 4); `SessionNotFound`, `SessionBusy`, `QueryFailed`, `DomainError` (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `SessionWorker(name, opener)` with `start(timeout=30.0)`, `connection`, `call(fn, timeout=120.0)`, `submit(command)`, `cancel(request_id)`, `set_emitter(emit)`, `clear_emitter(emit)`, `close()`, `last_activity`. `Emitter = Callable[[BaseModel], None]` receives `ServerEvent` models.
  - `SessionCreateRequest(profile_id, database=None)`, `SessionInfo(session_id, profile_id, database, server)`.
  - `SessionManager(connections, profiles, idle_timeout=600.0)` with `create(request) -> SessionInfo`, `worker(session_id)`, `attach(session_id, emit) -> SessionWorker`, `detach(session_id, emit)`, `close(session_id)`, `close_for_profile(profile_id)`, `reap_idle() -> int`, `close_all()`.
  - `MetadataService(registry, connections, profiles, idle_timeout=300.0)` with `children(request) -> list[ObjectNode]`, `detail(request) -> ObjectDetail`, `invalidate(profile_id)`, `reap_idle() -> int`, `close_all()`.

Event protocol rules (from the spec, implemented generically in the worker):
- `execute` → `started` → per result set: `result_set` (if it has columns) → `rows`* → `result_set_end(has_more)`; or `affected` for DML → `message`s as they arrive → `finished`.
- When `page_size` rows were sent and more exist, emit `result_set_end(has_more=True)` and **pause** (no `finished`). `fetch_more` continues; `discard` or a new `execute` closes the result and emits `finished`.
- Errors emit `error` then `finished`; cancellation emits a warning `message` then `finished(cancelled=True)`.
- Unexpected exceptions also end with `error` + `finished`, so the UI never hangs.
