`backend/tests/test_sessions.py`:
```python
import sqlite3
import threading
import time
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from dbaguy_backend.adapters.registry import AdapterRegistry
from dbaguy_backend.adapters.sqlite.adapter import SqliteAdapter
from dbaguy_backend.connectivity.ssh_tunnel import SshTunnelManager, write_askpass_script
from dbaguy_backend.domain.errors import SessionNotFound
from dbaguy_backend.domain.execution import DiscardCommand, ExecuteCommand, FetchMoreCommand
from dbaguy_backend.domain.profiles import ProfileUpsertRequest
from dbaguy_backend.services.connection_service import ConnectionService
from dbaguy_backend.services.profile_service import ProfileService
from dbaguy_backend.sessions.manager import SessionCreateRequest, SessionManager
from dbaguy_backend.storage.paths import profiles_file
from dbaguy_backend.storage.profile_store import ProfileStore
from dbaguy_backend.storage.secret_store import InMemorySecretStore

LONG_SQL = (
    "WITH RECURSIVE c(x) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT x + 1 FROM c WHERE x < 2000000000) SELECT count(*) FROM c"
)


class Recorder:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.events = []
        self._cv = threading.Condition()

    def __call__(self, event) -> None:
        with self._cv:
            self.events.append(event)
            self._cv.notify_all()

    def wait_for(self, predicate, timeout: float = 10.0):
        with self._cv:
            assert self._cv.wait_for(lambda: any(predicate(e) for e in self.events), timeout), self.types()
            return next(e for e in self.events if predicate(e))

    def of(self, request_id, type_):
        return [e for e in self.events if e.request_id == request_id and e.type == type_]

    def types(self):
        return [e.type for e in self.events]


@pytest.fixture
def setup(tmp_path):
    db = tmp_path / "s.db"
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
    conn.execute("CREATE TABLE t (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, v TEXT)")
    conn.executemany("INSERT INTO t (v) VALUES (?)", [(f"v{i}",) for i in range(1200)])
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    profiles = ProfileService(ProfileStore(profiles_file(tmp_path)), InMemorySecretStore())
    entry = profiles.create(ProfileUpsertRequest.model_validate({"profile": {"engine": "sqlite", "name": "s", "path": str(db)}}))
    connections = ConnectionService(AdapterRegistry([SqliteAdapter()]), profiles, SshTunnelManager(write_askpass_script(tmp_path)))
    manager = SessionManager(connections, profiles, idle_timeout=600)
    info = manager.create(SessionCreateRequest(profile_id=entry.profile.id))
    recorder = Recorder()
    worker = manager.attach(info.session_id, recorder)
    yield manager, info, worker, recorder
    manager.close_all()


def finished(rid):
    return lambda e: e.request_id == rid and e.type == "finished"


def paused(rid, count):
    return lambda e: e.request_id == rid and e.type == "result_set_end" and e.row_count == count


def test_paging_fetch_more_and_finish(setup):
    _, _, worker, rec = setup
    worker.submit(ExecuteCommand(request_id="r1", sql="SELECT id, v FROM t ORDER BY id", page_size=500))
    assert rec.wait_for(paused("r1", 500)).has_more
    worker.submit(FetchMoreCommand(request_id="r1", count=500))
    assert rec.wait_for(paused("r1", 1000)).has_more
    worker.submit(FetchMoreCommand(request_id="r1", count=500))
    rec.wait_for(finished("r1"))
    ends = [(e.row_count, e.has_more) for e in rec.of("r1", "result_set_end")]
    assert ends == [(500, True), (1000, True), (1200, False)]
    assert sum(len(e.rows) for e in rec.of("r1", "rows")) == 1200
    assert len(rec.of("r1", "result_set")) == 1


def test_exact_page_has_no_more(setup):
    _, _, worker, rec = setup
    worker.submit(ExecuteCommand(request_id="r2", sql="SELECT id FROM t LIMIT 500", page_size=500))
    rec.wait_for(finished("r2"))
    assert [(e.row_count, e.has_more) for e in rec.of("r2", "result_set_end")] == [(500, False)]


def test_dml_and_error(setup):
    _, _, worker, rec = setup
    worker.submit(ExecuteCommand(request_id="d1", sql="INSERT INTO t (v) VALUES ('x')"))
    rec.wait_for(finished("d1"))
    assert rec.of("d1", "affected")[0].rows == 1
    worker.submit(ExecuteCommand(request_id="e1", sql="SELECT * FROM nope"))
    rec.wait_for(finished("e1"))
    assert "no such table" in rec.of("e1", "error")[0].message


def test_cancel_running_query(setup):
    _, _, worker, rec = setup
    worker.submit(ExecuteCommand(request_id="c1", sql=LONG_SQL))
    rec.wait_for(lambda e: e.request_id == "c1" and e.type == "started")
    time.sleep(0.3)
    worker.cancel("c1")
    done = rec.wait_for(finished("c1"), timeout=5)
    assert done.cancelled is True


def test_discard_paused_then_reuse(setup):
    _, _, worker, rec = setup
    worker.submit(ExecuteCommand(request_id="p1", sql="SELECT id FROM t", page_size=10))
    rec.wait_for(paused("p1", 10))
    worker.submit(DiscardCommand(request_id="p1"))
    rec.wait_for(finished("p1"))
    worker.submit(ExecuteCommand(request_id="p2", sql="SELECT 1"))
    rec.wait_for(finished("p2"))
    assert rec.of("p2", "rows")[0].rows == [[1]]


def test_new_execute_discards_paused_result(setup):
    _, _, worker, rec = setup
    worker.submit(ExecuteCommand(request_id="a", sql="SELECT id FROM t", page_size=10))
    rec.wait_for(paused("a", 10))
    worker.submit(ExecuteCommand(request_id="b", sql="SELECT 2"))
    rec.wait_for(finished("b"))
    assert rec.of("a", "finished")  # the paused request was finished first


def test_call_runs_on_worker_thread(setup):
    _, _, worker, _ = setup
    assert worker.call(lambda c: threading.current_thread().name).startswith("session-")


def test_reap_idle_closes_detached_sessions(setup):
    manager, info, _, rec = setup
    manager.detach(info.session_id, rec)
    manager._idle_timeout = 0
    assert manager.reap_idle() == 1
    with pytest.raises(SessionNotFound):
        manager.worker(info.session_id)
```

`backend/tests/test_metadata_service.py`:
```python
import psycopg
import pytest

from dbaguy_backend.adapters.postgres.adapter import PostgresAdapter
from dbaguy_backend.adapters.registry import AdapterRegistry
from dbaguy_backend.connectivity.ssh_tunnel import SshTunnelManager, write_askpass_script
from dbaguy_backend.domain.metadata import MetadataRequest, PathSegment
from dbaguy_backend.domain.profiles import ProfileSecretsInput, ProfileUpsertRequest
from dbaguy_backend.services.connection_service import ConnectionService
from dbaguy_backend.services.metadata_service import MetadataService
from dbaguy_backend.services.profile_service import ProfileService
from dbaguy_backend.storage.paths import profiles_file
from dbaguy_backend.storage.profile_store import ProfileStore
from dbaguy_backend.storage.secret_store import InMemorySecretStore
from tests.support import PG_HOST, PG_PASSWORD, PG_PORT, PG_USER, requires_pg

pytestmark = requires_pg


@pytest.fixture
def service_and_profile(tmp_path):
    profiles = ProfileService(ProfileStore(profiles_file(tmp_path)), InMemorySecretStore())
    entry = profiles.create(ProfileUpsertRequest.model_validate({
        "profile": {"engine": "postgres", "name": "pg", "host": PG_HOST, "port": PG_PORT, "database": "dbaguy",
                    "username": PG_USER, "ssl": {"mode": "disable"}},
        "secrets": ProfileSecretsInput(password=PG_PASSWORD).model_dump(),
    }))
    registry = AdapterRegistry([PostgresAdapter()])
    connections = ConnectionService(registry, profiles, SshTunnelManager(write_askpass_script(tmp_path)))
    service = MetadataService(registry, connections, profiles)
    yield service, entry.profile.id
    service.close_all()


def seg(kind, name):
    return PathSegment(kind=kind, name=name)


def test_routes_each_database_to_its_own_connection(service_and_profile):
    service, pid = service_and_profile
    databases = [n.name for n in service.children(MetadataRequest(profile_id=pid))]
    assert "analytics" in databases
    path = [seg("database", "analytics"), seg("schema", "public"), seg("folder", "tables")]
    assert [n.name for n in service.children(MetadataRequest(profile_id=pid, path=path))] == ["page_views"]


def test_reconnects_after_server_drops_connection(service_and_profile):
    service, pid = service_and_profile
    request = MetadataRequest(profile_id=pid, path=[seg("database", "dbaguy")])
    assert service.children(request)
    with psycopg.connect(host=PG_HOST, port=PG_PORT, user=PG_USER, password=PG_PASSWORD, dbname="dbaguy",
                         autocommit=True, application_name="pytest-admin") as admin:
        admin.execute("SELECT pg_terminate_backend(pid) FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE application_name = 'DbaGuy'")
    assert service.children(request)  # transparently reconnected
```

`backend/dbaguy_backend/sessions/worker.py`:
```python
import queue
import threading
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from concurrent.futures import Future
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, TypeVar

from loguru import logger
from pydantic import BaseModel

from ..adapters.base import DatabaseConnection, StatementCancelled, StatementError, StatementResult
from ..domain.errors import ConnectionFailed, SessionBusy
from ..domain.execution import (
    AffectedEvent,
    CancelCommand,
    DiscardCommand,
    ErrorEvent,
    ExecuteCommand,
    FetchMoreCommand,
    FinishedEvent,
    MessageEvent,
    MessageSeverity,
    ResultSetEndEvent,
    ResultSetEvent,
    RowsEvent,
    StartedEvent,
)

T = TypeVar("T")
Emitter = Callable[[BaseModel], None]
_CHUNK = 200
_STOP = object()


def _discard_event(_: BaseModel) -> None:
    return None


@dataclass
class _OpenExecution:
    request_id: str
    result: StatementResult
    started: float
    index: int = 0
    sent: int = 0
    columns_sent: bool = False
    peeked: list[list[Any]] = field(default_factory=list)


class SessionWorker:
    """Owns one connection and one thread; every driver call happens on that thread."""

    def __init__(self, name: str, opener: Callable[[], Any]) -> None:
        self.name = name
        self._opener = opener
        self._queue: queue.Queue = queue.Queue()
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, name=f"session-{name}", daemon=True)
        self._lease: Any = None
        self._open_error: BaseException | None = None
        self._opened = threading.Event()
        self._emit: Emitter = _discard_event
        self._emit_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._state_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._running_request: str | None = None
        self._open_exec: _OpenExecution | None = None
        self._closed = False
        self.last_activity = time.monotonic()

    # ---- lifecycle (any thread) ----

    def start(self, timeout: float = 30.0) -> None:
        self._thread.start()
        if not self._opened.wait(timeout):
            raise ConnectionFailed(f"Timed out opening a connection for {self.name}")
        if self._open_error is not None:
            raise self._open_error

    @property
    def connection(self) -> DatabaseConnection:
        if self._lease is None:
            raise ConnectionFailed("Connection is not open")
        return self._lease.connection

    def set_emitter(self, emit: Emitter) -> None:
        with self._emit_lock:
            self._emit = emit

    def clear_emitter(self, emit: Emitter) -> None:
        with self._emit_lock:
            if self._emit is emit:
                self._emit = _discard_event

    def call(self, fn: Callable[[DatabaseConnection], T], timeout: float | None = 120.0) -> T:
        """Run fn(connection) on the worker thread and wait for its result."""
        future: Future[T] = Future()

        def task() -> None:
            if not future.set_running_or_notify_cancel():
                return
            try:
                self._discard_open()
                future.set_result(fn(self.connection))
            except BaseException as exc:
                future.set_exception(exc)

        self._put(task)
        return future.result(timeout)

    def submit(self, command: BaseModel) -> None:
        match command:
            case ExecuteCommand():
                self._put(lambda: self._execute(command))
            case FetchMoreCommand():
                self._put(lambda: self._fetch_more(command))
            case DiscardCommand():
                self._put(lambda: self._discard(command.request_id))
            case CancelCommand():
                self.cancel(command.request_id)

    def cancel(self, request_id: str) -> None:
        with self._state_lock:
            running = self._running_request == request_id
        if running:
            logger.info("Cancelling request {} on {}", request_id, self.name)
            self.connection.cancel()
        else:
            self._put(lambda: self._discard(request_id))

    def close(self) -> None:
        if self._closed:
            return
        self._closed = True
        with self._state_lock:
            running = self._running_request is not None
        if running and self._lease is not None:
            self.connection.cancel()
        self._queue.put(_STOP)
        self._thread.join(timeout=10)

    def _put(self, task: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
        if self._closed:
            raise SessionBusy("Session is closed")
        self.last_activity = time.monotonic()
        self._queue.put(task)

    # ---- worker thread ----

    def _run(self) -> None:
        try:
            self._lease = self._opener()
        except BaseException as exc:
            self._open_error = exc
            self._opened.set()
            return
        self._opened.set()
        while True:
            task = self._queue.get()
            if task is _STOP:
                break
            try:
                task()
            except Exception:
                logger.exception("Session task failed on {}", self.name)
        self._discard_open()
        self._lease.close()
        logger.info("Session {} closed", self.name)

    def _send(self, event: BaseModel) -> None:
        with self._emit_lock:
            emit = self._emit
        try:
            emit(event)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("Failed to deliver event on {}", self.name)

    def _set_running(self, request_id: str | None) -> None:
        with self._state_lock:
            self._running_request = request_id

    def _in_transaction(self) -> bool:
        try:
            return self.connection.in_transaction()
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _execute(self, cmd: ExecuteCommand) -> None:
        self._discard_open()
        started = time.monotonic()
        self._set_running(cmd.request_id)
        self._send(StartedEvent(request_id=cmd.request_id))
        logger.debug("[{}] execute {}: {}", self.name, cmd.request_id, cmd.sql[:2000])
        try:
            result = self.connection.run(cmd.sql)
            self._open_exec = _OpenExecution(request_id=cmd.request_id, result=result, started=started)
            self._pump(self._open_exec, cmd.page_size)
        except StatementError as exc:
            self._fail(cmd.request_id, started, exc)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Unexpected error executing on {}", self.name)
            self._fail(cmd.request_id, started, StatementError(f"Internal error: {exc}"))
        finally:
            self._set_running(None)

    def _fetch_more(self, cmd: FetchMoreCommand) -> None:
        execution = self._open_exec
        if execution is None or execution.request_id != cmd.request_id:
            self._send(ErrorEvent(request_id=cmd.request_id, message="No open result to fetch from", code="no_open_result"))
            return
        self._set_running(cmd.request_id)
        try:
            self._pump(execution, cmd.count)
        except StatementError as exc:
            self._fail(cmd.request_id, execution.started, exc)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Unexpected error fetching on {}", self.name)
            self._fail(cmd.request_id, execution.started, StatementError(f"Internal error: {exc}"))
        finally:
            self._set_running(None)

    def _discard(self, request_id: str) -> None:
        if self._open_exec is not None and self._open_exec.request_id == request_id:
            self._discard_open()

    def _discard_open(self) -> None:
        if self._open_exec is not None:
            self._finish(self._open_exec)

    def _send_messages(self, ex: _OpenExecution) -> None:
        for message in ex.result.take_messages():
            self._send(MessageEvent(request_id=ex.request_id, severity=message.severity, text=message.text))

    def _pump(self, ex: _OpenExecution, budget: int) -> None:
        result = ex.result
        while True:
            columns = result.columns()
            if columns is None:
                affected = result.affected_rows()
                if affected is not None:
                    self._send(AffectedEvent(request_id=ex.request_id, index=ex.index, rows=affected))
            else:
                if not ex.columns_sent:
                    self._send(ResultSetEvent(request_id=ex.request_id, index=ex.index, columns=columns))
                    ex.columns_sent = True
                while budget > 0:
                    want = min(budget, _CHUNK)
                    rows, ex.peeked = ex.peeked[:want], ex.peeked[want:]
                    if len(rows) < want:
                        rows += result.fetch(want - len(rows))
                    if rows:
                        self._send(RowsEvent(request_id=ex.request_id, index=ex.index, rows=rows))
                        ex.sent += len(rows)
                        budget -= len(rows)
                    self._send_messages(ex)
                    if len(rows) < want:
                        break  # result set exhausted
                else:
                    if not ex.peeked:
                        ex.peeked = result.fetch(1)
                    if ex.peeked:  # page full and more rows exist: pause
                        self._send(ResultSetEndEvent(request_id=ex.request_id, index=ex.index, row_count=ex.sent, has_more=True))
                        return
                self._send(ResultSetEndEvent(request_id=ex.request_id, index=ex.index, row_count=ex.sent, has_more=False))
            self._send_messages(ex)
            if not result.next_result():
                break
            ex.index += 1
            ex.sent = 0
            ex.columns_sent = False
            ex.peeked = []
        self._finish(ex)

    def _finish(self, ex: _OpenExecution, cancelled: bool = False) -> None:
        self._open_exec = None
        try:
            ex.result.close()
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Error closing result on {}: {}", self.name, exc)
        self._send(FinishedEvent(
            request_id=ex.request_id,
            elapsed_ms=int((time.monotonic() - ex.started) * 1000),
            in_transaction=self._in_transaction(),
            cancelled=cancelled,
        ))

    def _fail(self, request_id: str, started: float, exc: StatementError) -> None:
        if self._open_exec is not None:
            try:
                self._open_exec.result.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self._open_exec = None
        cancelled = isinstance(exc, StatementCancelled)
        if cancelled:
            self._send(MessageEvent(request_id=request_id, severity=MessageSeverity.WARNING, text=exc.message))
        else:
            self._send(ErrorEvent(request_id=request_id, message=exc.message, code=exc.code, position=exc.position))
        self._send(FinishedEvent(
            request_id=request_id,
            elapsed_ms=int((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000),
            in_transaction=self._in_transaction(),
            cancelled=cancelled,
        ))
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the session manager and metadata service**

`backend/dbaguy_backend/sessions/manager.py`:
```python
import threading
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from loguru import logger
from pydantic import BaseModel

from ..domain.errors import SessionNotFound
from ..domain.metadata import ServerInfo
from ..services.connection_service import ConnectionService
from ..services.profile_service import ProfileService
from .worker import Emitter, SessionWorker


class SessionCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    profile_id: UUID
    database: str | None = None


class SessionInfo(BaseModel):
    session_id: str
    profile_id: UUID
    database: str | None
    server: ServerInfo


@dataclass
class _Entry:
    worker: SessionWorker
    info: SessionInfo
    attached: int = 0
    detached_at: float = 0.0


class SessionManager:
    """User sessions (one per editor tab). Closed explicitly or after idling without a WebSocket."""

    def __init__(self, connections: ConnectionService, profiles: ProfileService, idle_timeout: float = 600.0) -> None:
        self._connections = connections
        self._profiles = profiles
        self._idle_timeout = idle_timeout
        self._sessions: dict[str, _Entry] = {}
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def create(self, request: SessionCreateRequest) -> SessionInfo:
        profile = self._profiles.get(request.profile_id)
        secrets = self._profiles.resolve_secrets(profile)
        session_id = uuid4().hex
        worker = SessionWorker(
            f"{profile.name}:{session_id[:8]}",
            lambda: self._connections.open(profile, request.database, secrets),
        )
        worker.start()
        try:
            server = worker.call(lambda c: c.server_info())
        except BaseException:
            worker.close()
            raise
        info = SessionInfo(session_id=session_id, profile_id=profile.id, database=server.current_database, server=server)
        with self._lock:
            self._sessions[session_id] = _Entry(worker=worker, info=info, detached_at=time.monotonic())
        logger.info("Opened session {} for '{}'", session_id, profile.name)
        return info

    def _entry(self, session_id: str) -> _Entry:
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._sessions.get(session_id)
        if entry is None:
            raise SessionNotFound(f"Session {session_id} not found")
        return entry

    def worker(self, session_id: str) -> SessionWorker:
        return self._entry(session_id).worker

    def attach(self, session_id: str, emit: Emitter) -> SessionWorker:
        entry = self._entry(session_id)
        with self._lock:
            entry.attached += 1
        entry.worker.set_emitter(emit)
        return entry.worker

    def detach(self, session_id: str, emit: Emitter) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._sessions.get(session_id)
            if entry is None:
                return
            entry.attached = max(0, entry.attached - 1)
            if entry.attached == 0:
                entry.detached_at = time.monotonic()
        entry.worker.clear_emitter(emit)

    def close(self, session_id: str) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._sessions.pop(session_id, None)
        if entry is None:
            raise SessionNotFound(f"Session {session_id} not found")
        entry.worker.close()
        logger.info("Closed session {}", session_id)

    def close_for_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            ids = [sid for sid, e in self._sessions.items() if e.info.profile_id == profile_id]
        for sid in ids:
            self.close(sid)

    def reap_idle(self) -> int:
        now = time.monotonic()
        with self._lock:
            idle = [
                sid for sid, e in self._sessions.items()
                if e.attached == 0 and now - e.detached_at >= self._idle_timeout
            ]
        for sid in idle:
            logger.info("Closing idle session {}", sid)
            self.close(sid)
        return len(idle)

    def close_all(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            ids = list(self._sessions)
        for sid in ids:
            self.close(sid)
```

`backend/dbaguy_backend/services/metadata_service.py`:
```python
import threading
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import TypeVar
from uuid import UUID

from loguru import logger

from ..adapters.base import DatabaseConnection, StatementError
from ..adapters.registry import AdapterRegistry
from ..domain.errors import DomainError, QueryFailed
from ..domain.metadata import MetadataRequest, NodeKind, ObjectDetail, ObjectNode
from ..domain.profiles import AnyProfile
from ..sessions.worker import SessionWorker
from .connection_service import ConnectionService
from .profile_service import ProfileService

T = TypeVar("T")
_Key = tuple[UUID, str | None]


@dataclass
class _MetaEntry:
    worker: SessionWorker
    last_used: float


class MetadataService:
    """Object-tree queries on dedicated metadata connections, one per (profile, database)."""

    def __init__(
        self, registry: AdapterRegistry, connections: ConnectionService, profiles: ProfileService,
        idle_timeout: float = 300.0,
    ) -> None:
        self._registry = registry
        self._connections = connections
        self._profiles = profiles
        self._idle_timeout = idle_timeout
        self._workers: dict[_Key, _MetaEntry] = {}
        self._key_locks: dict[_Key, threading.Lock] = {}
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def children(self, request: MetadataRequest) -> list[ObjectNode]:
        return self._call(request, lambda c: c.list_children(request.path))

    def detail(self, request: MetadataRequest) -> ObjectDetail:
        return self._call(request, lambda c: c.object_detail(request.path))

    def invalidate(self, profile_id: UUID) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            keys = [k for k in self._workers if k[0] == profile_id]
        for key in keys:
            self._drop(key)

    def reap_idle(self) -> int:
        now = time.monotonic()
        with self._lock:
            idle = [k for k, e in self._workers.items() if now - e.last_used >= self._idle_timeout]
        for key in idle:
            self._drop(key)
        return len(idle)

    def close_all(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            keys = list(self._workers)
        for key in keys:
            self._drop(key)

    def _call(self, request: MetadataRequest, fn: Callable[[DatabaseConnection], T]) -> T:
        profile = self._profiles.get(request.profile_id)
        adapter = self._registry.get(profile.engine)
        database = None
        if adapter.capabilities.per_database_connection and request.path and request.path[0].kind == NodeKind.DATABASE:
            database = request.path[0].name
        key = (profile.id, database)
        for attempt in (1, 2):
            worker = self._worker(key, profile, database)
            try:
                return worker.call(fn)
            except DomainError:
                raise  # NotFound / ValidationFailed from introspection: the connection is fine
            except Exception as exc:
                self._drop(key)
                if attempt == 2:
                    message = exc.message if isinstance(exc, StatementError) else str(exc)
                    raise QueryFailed(message) from exc
                logger.warning("Metadata query failed on {} ({}); reconnecting", profile.name, exc)
        raise AssertionError("unreachable")

    def _worker(self, key: _Key, profile: AnyProfile, database: str | None) -> SessionWorker:
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._workers.get(key)
            if entry is not None:
                entry.last_used = time.monotonic()
                return entry.worker
            key_lock = self._key_locks.setdefault(key, threading.Lock())
        with key_lock:  # one opener per key; other profiles are not blocked
            with self._lock:
                entry = self._workers.get(key)
                if entry is not None:
                    return entry.worker
            secrets = self._profiles.resolve_secrets(profile)
            worker = SessionWorker(
                f"meta:{profile.name}:{database or '-'}",
                lambda: self._connections.open(profile, database, secrets),
            )
            worker.start()
            with self._lock:
                self._workers[key] = _MetaEntry(worker=worker, last_used=time.monotonic())
            return worker

    def _drop(self, key: _Key) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            entry = self._workers.pop(key, None)
        if entry is not None:
            entry.worker.close()
```

### Task 11: HTTP + WebSocket API and service wiring

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/dbaguy_backend/api/profiles.py`, `backend/dbaguy_backend/api/metadata.py`, `backend/dbaguy_backend/api/sessions.py`
- Modify: `backend/dbaguy_backend/container.py` (build all services, housekeeping thread), `backend/dbaguy_backend/app.py` (include routers), `backend/dbaguy_backend/api/system.py` (add `/system/engines`), `backend/dbaguy_backend/connectivity/ssh_tunnel.py` (write the askpass script lazily when missing)
- Regenerate: `src/lib/api/openapi.json`, `src/lib/api/schema.d.ts`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_api.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every service from Tasks 3–10.
- Produces HTTP endpoints (all require the bearer token):

| Method | Path | Body | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/system/health` | — | `HealthResponse` |
| GET | `/system/engines` | — | `EngineInfo[]` |
| GET | `/profiles` | — | `ProfileEntry[]` |
| POST | `/profiles` | `ProfileUpsertRequest` | `ProfileEntry` (201) |
| PUT | `/profiles/{profile_id}` | `ProfileUpsertRequest` | `ProfileEntry` |
| DELETE | `/profiles/{profile_id}` | — | 204 |
| POST | `/profiles/{profile_id}/unlock` | `ProfileSecretsInput` | 204 |
| POST | `/profiles/import-url` | `ImportUrlRequest` | `ImportUrlResponse` |
| POST | `/profiles/test` | `ConnectionTestRequest` | `ConnectionTestResult` |
| POST | `/metadata/children` | `MetadataRequest` | `ObjectNode[]` |
| POST | `/metadata/detail` | `MetadataRequest` | `ObjectDetail` |
| POST | `/sessions` | `SessionCreateRequest` | `SessionInfo` (201) |
| DELETE | `/sessions/{session_id}` | — | 204 |
| GET | `/sessions/_schema/execution` | — | `ExecutionSchema` (schema only; always 404) |

- WebSocket `/sessions/{session_id}/ws?token=…`: receives `ClientCommand` JSON, sends `ServerEvent` JSON. Bad token → close code 4401; unknown session → 4404; invalid command → `error` event with `code="invalid_command"`.
- `AppContainer(settings, secret_store=None, registry=None)` exposes `.registry, .profiles, .tunnels, .connections, .sessions, .metadata`; `default_registry()`.

```python
import sqlite3

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from starlette.websockets import WebSocketDisconnect

from dbaguy_backend.app import create_app
from dbaguy_backend.config import Settings
from dbaguy_backend.container import AppContainer
from dbaguy_backend.storage.secret_store import InMemorySecretStore

TOKEN = "test-token"
AUTH = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {TOKEN}"}


@pytest.fixture
def client(tmp_path):

@pytest.fixture
def sqlite_profile_id(client, tmp_path):

    body = {"profile": {"engine": "sqlite", "name": "local", "path": str(db)}}
    return client.post("/profiles", json=body, headers=AUTH).json()["profile"]["id"]


def test_profile_crud_and_secret_not_on_disk(client, tmp_path):
    body = {"profile": {"engine": "sqlite", "name": "local", "path": "/tmp/x.db"}, "secrets": {"password": "s3cret-xyz"}}
    created = client.post("/profiles", json=body, headers=AUTH)
    assert created.status_code == 201 and created.json()["has_password"] is True
    pid = created.json()["profile"]["id"]
    assert "s3cret-xyz" not in (tmp_path / "data" / "profiles.json").read_text()
    assert [e["profile"]["name"] for e in client.get("/profiles", headers=AUTH).json()] == ["local"]
    body["profile"]["name"] = "renamed"
    assert client.put(f"/profiles/{pid}", json=body, headers=AUTH).json()["profile"]["name"] == "renamed"
    assert client.delete(f"/profiles/{pid}", headers=AUTH).status_code == 204
    missing = client.delete(f"/profiles/{pid}", headers=AUTH)
    assert missing.status_code == 404 and missing.json()["error"]["code"] == "profile_not_found"


def test_import_url_and_validation(client):
    response = client.post("/profiles/import-url", json={"url": "postgres://me:p%40ss@db:5433/app?sslmode=require"}, headers=AUTH)
    assert response.json()["profile"]["port"] == 5433 and response.json()["password"] == "p@ss"
    assert client.post("/profiles/import-url", json={"url": "redis://x"}, headers=AUTH).status_code == 422
    invalid = client.post("/profiles", json={"profile": {"engine": "sqlite"}}, headers=AUTH)
    assert invalid.status_code == 422 and invalid.json()["error"]["code"] == "validation_failed"


def test_engines(client):
    assert {e["engine"] for e in client.get("/system/engines", headers=AUTH).json()} == {"postgres", "sqlite"}


def test_metadata_and_session_over_websocket(client, sqlite_profile_id):
    pid = sqlite_profile_id
    nodes = client.post("/metadata/children", json={"profile_id": pid, "path": []}, headers=AUTH).json()
    assert nodes[0]["name"] == "main"
    tables_path = [{"kind": "schema", "name": "main"}, {"kind": "folder", "name": "tables"}]
    tables = client.post("/metadata/children", json={"profile_id": pid, "path": tables_path}, headers=AUTH).json()
    detail = client.post("/metadata/detail", json={"profile_id": pid, "path": tables[0]["path"]}, headers=AUTH).json()
    assert detail["ddl"].startswith("CREATE TABLE t")

    session = client.post("/sessions", json={"profile_id": pid}, headers=AUTH).json()
    assert session["server"]["engine"] == "sqlite"
    sid = session["session_id"]
    with client.websocket_connect(f"/sessions/{sid}/ws?token={TOKEN}") as ws:
        ws.send_json({"type": "execute", "request_id": "q1", "sql": "SELECT id, v FROM t ORDER BY id"})
        events = []
        while True:
            events.append(ws.receive_json())
            if events[-1]["type"] == "finished":
                break
        assert [e["type"] for e in events] == ["started", "result_set", "rows", "result_set_end", "finished"]
        assert events[2]["rows"] == [[1, "a"], [2, "b"], [3, "c"]]
        ws.send_json({"type": "bogus", "request_id": "x"})
        assert ws.receive_json()["code"] == "invalid_command"
    assert client.delete(f"/sessions/{sid}", headers=AUTH).status_code == 204


def test_websocket_rejects_bad_token(client, sqlite_profile_id):
    sid = client.post("/sessions", json={"profile_id": sqlite_profile_id}, headers=AUTH).json()["session_id"]
    with pytest.raises(WebSocketDisconnect):
        with client.websocket_connect(f"/sessions/{sid}/ws?token=bad") as ws:
            ws.receive_json()


def test_metadata_unknown_profile(client):
    response = client.post("/metadata/children", json={"profile_id": "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000", "path": []}, headers=AUTH)
    assert response.status_code == 404
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Wire the container**

Replace `backend/dbaguy_backend/container.py`:
```python
import threading

from loguru import logger

from .adapters.postgres.adapter import PostgresAdapter
from .adapters.registry import AdapterRegistry
from .adapters.sqlite.adapter import SqliteAdapter
from .config import Settings
from .connectivity.ssh_tunnel import SshTunnelManager
from .services.connection_service import ConnectionService
from .services.metadata_service import MetadataService
from .services.profile_service import ProfileService
from .sessions.manager import SessionManager
from .storage.paths import profiles_file
from .storage.profile_store import ProfileStore
from .storage.secret_store import KeyringSecretStore, SecretStore

_HOUSEKEEPING_INTERVAL_S = 30


def default_registry() -> AdapterRegistry:
    return AdapterRegistry([PostgresAdapter(), SqliteAdapter()])


class AppContainer:
    """Builds and owns every long-lived service."""

    def __init__(
        self, settings: Settings, secret_store: SecretStore | None = None, registry: AdapterRegistry | None = None
    ) -> None:
        self.settings = settings
        self.registry = registry or default_registry()
        self.profiles = ProfileService(ProfileStore(profiles_file(settings.data_dir)), secret_store or KeyringSecretStore())
        self.tunnels = SshTunnelManager(settings.data_dir / "bin" / "askpass.sh")
        self.connections = ConnectionService(self.registry, self.profiles, self.tunnels)
        self.sessions = SessionManager(self.connections, self.profiles)
        self.metadata = MetadataService(self.registry, self.connections, self.profiles)
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._housekeeper: threading.Thread | None = None

    def start(self) -> None:
        self._housekeeper = threading.Thread(target=self._housekeep, name="housekeeping", daemon=True)
        self._housekeeper.start()

    def _housekeep(self) -> None:
        while not self._stop.wait(_HOUSEKEEPING_INTERVAL_S):
            try:
                self.sessions.reap_idle()
                self.metadata.reap_idle()
            except Exception:
                logger.exception("Housekeeping failed")

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()
        self.sessions.close_all()
        self.metadata.close_all()
        self.tunnels.close_all()
```

In `backend/dbaguy_backend/connectivity/ssh_tunnel.py`, at the start of `SshTunnelManager._start` (after the `ssh.host` check), add:

```python
            write_askpass_script(self._askpass.parent)
```
(`write_askpass_script(dir)` always writes `dir / "askpass.sh"`, which matches the container's path.)

```python
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Response

from ..container import AppContainer
from ..domain.profiles import (
    ConnectionTestRequest,
    ConnectionTestResult,
    ImportUrlRequest,
    ImportUrlResponse,
    ProfileEntry,
    ProfileSecretsInput,
    ProfileUpsertRequest,
)
from .deps import get_container, require_token

router = APIRouter(prefix="/profiles", tags=["profiles"], dependencies=[Depends(require_token)])


@router.get("", response_model=list[ProfileEntry])
def list_profiles(container: AppContainer = Depends(get_container)) -> list[ProfileEntry]:
    return container.profiles.list_entries()


@router.post("", response_model=ProfileEntry, status_code=201)
def create_profile(body: ProfileUpsertRequest, container: AppContainer = Depends(get_container)) -> ProfileEntry:
    return container.profiles.create(body)


@router.put("/{profile_id}", response_model=ProfileEntry)
def update_profile(
    profile_id: UUID, body: ProfileUpsertRequest, container: AppContainer = Depends(get_container)
) -> ProfileEntry:
    entry = container.profiles.update(profile_id, body)
    container.metadata.invalidate(profile_id)
    return entry


@router.delete("/{profile_id}", status_code=204)
def delete_profile(profile_id: UUID, container: AppContainer = Depends(get_container)) -> Response:
    container.profiles.get(profile_id)  # 404 before touching sessions
    container.sessions.close_for_profile(profile_id)
    container.metadata.invalidate(profile_id)
    container.profiles.delete(profile_id)
    return Response(status_code=204)


@router.post("/{profile_id}/unlock", status_code=204)
def unlock_profile(
    profile_id: UUID, body: ProfileSecretsInput, container: AppContainer = Depends(get_container)
) -> Response:
    container.profiles.unlock(profile_id, body)
    return Response(status_code=204)


@router.post("/import-url", response_model=ImportUrlResponse)
def import_url(body: ImportUrlRequest, container: AppContainer = Depends(get_container)) -> ImportUrlResponse:
    parsed = container.registry.parse_url(body.url)
    return ImportUrlResponse(profile=parsed.profile, password=parsed.password)


@router.post("/test", response_model=ConnectionTestResult)
def test_connection(body: ConnectionTestRequest, container: AppContainer = Depends(get_container)) -> ConnectionTestResult:
    return container.connections.test(body)
```

`backend/dbaguy_backend/api/sessions.py`:
```python
import asyncio
from contextlib import suppress
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Response, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from loguru import logger
from pydantic import BaseModel, TypeAdapter, ValidationError

from ..container import AppContainer
from ..domain.errors import DomainError, NotFound, SessionNotFound
from ..domain.execution import CancelCommand, ClientCommand, ErrorEvent, ExecutionSchema
from ..sessions.manager import SessionCreateRequest, SessionInfo
from .deps import get_container, require_token, websocket_token_ok

router = APIRouter(prefix="/sessions", tags=["sessions"])
_COMMANDS: TypeAdapter[Any] = TypeAdapter(ClientCommand)


@router.post("", response_model=SessionInfo, status_code=201, dependencies=[Depends(require_token)])
def create_session(body: SessionCreateRequest, container: AppContainer = Depends(get_container)) -> SessionInfo:
    return container.sessions.create(body)


@router.delete("/{session_id}", status_code=204, dependencies=[Depends(require_token)])
def close_session(session_id: str, container: AppContainer = Depends(get_container)) -> Response:
    container.sessions.close(session_id)
    return Response(status_code=204)


@router.get("/_schema/execution", response_model=ExecutionSchema, dependencies=[Depends(require_token)])
def execution_schema() -> ExecutionSchema:
    """Never called; exposes the WebSocket protocol models in OpenAPI."""
    raise NotFound("Schema-only endpoint")


@router.websocket("/{session_id}/ws")
async def session_socket(websocket: WebSocket, session_id: str) -> None:
    container: AppContainer = websocket.app.state.container
    if not websocket_token_ok(websocket):
        await websocket.close(code=4401)
        return
    try:
        container.sessions.worker(session_id)
    except SessionNotFound:
        await websocket.close(code=4404)
        return
    await websocket.accept()
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    outbox: asyncio.Queue[dict[str, Any] | None] = asyncio.Queue()

    def emit(event: BaseModel) -> None:  # called from the worker thread
        loop.call_soon_threadsafe(outbox.put_nowait, event.model_dump(mode="json"))

    async def sender() -> None:
        while (item := await outbox.get()) is not None:
            await websocket.send_json(item)

    worker = container.sessions.attach(session_id, emit)
    send_task = asyncio.create_task(sender())
    logger.info("WebSocket attached to session {}", session_id)
    try:
        while True:
            raw = await websocket.receive_json()
            try:
                command = _COMMANDS.validate_python(raw)
            except ValidationError as exc:
                request_id = str(raw.get("request_id", "")) if isinstance(raw, dict) else ""
                error = ErrorEvent(request_id=request_id, message=f"Invalid command: {exc.errors()[0]['msg']}", code="invalid_command")
                await outbox.put(error.model_dump(mode="json"))
                continue
            try:
                if isinstance(command, CancelCommand):
                    await asyncio.to_thread(worker.cancel, command.request_id)  # may block for a moment
                else:
                    worker.submit(command)
            except DomainError as exc:
                await outbox.put(ErrorEvent(request_id=command.request_id, message=exc.message, code=exc.code).model_dump(mode="json"))
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        pass
    finally:
        container.sessions.detach(session_id, emit)
        await outbox.put(None)
        with suppress(Exception):
            await send_task
        logger.info("WebSocket detached from session {}", session_id)
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `src/App.tsx` (render `AppShell`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `api()`, `unwrap()`, `ApiError`, `getBackendInfo()`, `restartBackend()`, `isTauriRuntime()` (Task 2); HTTP API (Task 11).
- Produces:
  - `src/lib/api/types.ts`: `ProfileEntry, Profile, ServerProfile, SqliteProfile, ProfileDraft, ServerProfileDraft, SqliteProfileDraft, ProfileSecretsInput, ConnectionTestResult, EngineInfo, Engine, PathSegment, ObjectNode, ObjectDetail, ColumnInfo, SessionInfo, ServerEvent, ClientCommand, ColumnMeta`; `pathKey(path: PathSegment[]): string`.
  - `cn(...classes)` (clsx wrapper).
  - UI primitives: `Button({variant: "primary"|"secondary"|"ghost"|"danger", size: "sm"|"md"})`, `Input`, `Select`, `Checkbox`, `Label`, `FieldRow`, `Dialog({open, onOpenChange, title, children, footer, width})`, `Tabs({value, onValueChange, items: {value,label}[]})` + `TabPanel`.
  - Zustand `useWorkspace` with state `{tabs: Tab[], activeTabId, selectedProfileId}` and actions `openObject(profileId, node, profileName)`, `openConsole(profileId, database, profileName)`, `closeTab(id)`, `activate(id)`, `selectProfile(id)`; `Tab = ObjectTab | ConsoleTab`.
  - Hooks: `useProfiles()`, `useEngines()`, `useSaveProfile()`, `useDeleteProfile()`, `useTestConnection()`, `useImportUrl()`, `useUnlockProfile()`.
  - `ConnectionDialog({open, onOpenChange, entry?: ProfileEntry})`, `UnlockDialog({profile, open, onOpenChange, onUnlocked})`.
  - `buildFolderTree(entries) -> FolderNode` (`{name, path, folders: FolderNode[], profiles: ProfileEntry[]}`).
  - `AppShell` layout: 38 px overlay title bar (`data-tauri-drag-region`, 80 px left padding for traffic lights) → horizontal resizable panels: sidebar (default 22%, min 15%) | workspace → 22 px status bar. Sidebar and workspace slots are filled in Tasks 13–14 (`<Explorer />`, `<Workspace />`); until then render placeholders.

UI conventions (apply to every component in Tasks 12–14):
- Dense desktop UI: 13 px text, 24–26 px control height, 22 px tree rows, 1 px `border-border` separators, `bg-panel` for sidebars/toolbars, `bg-bg` for content, `bg-hover` hover, `bg-selected` selection, `text-muted` secondary text. No shadows except dialogs.
- Native-feeling interactions: no text selection on chrome (`user-select: none` from Task 2 CSS) but selectable DDL/results (`data-selectable`); macOS shortcuts (⌘N new connection, ⌘W close tab, ⌘↵ run).
- Every async action shows its pending state and surfaces `ApiError.message` inline (no silent failures).

```ts
import type { components } from "./schema";

type S = components["schemas"];

export type ProfileEntry = S["ProfileEntry"];
export type ServerProfile = S["ServerProfile"];
export type SqliteProfile = S["SqliteProfile"];
export type Profile = ServerProfile | SqliteProfile;
export type ServerProfileDraft = S["ServerProfileDraft"];
export type SqliteProfileDraft = S["SqliteProfileDraft"];
export type ProfileDraft = ServerProfileDraft | SqliteProfileDraft;
export type ProfileSecretsInput = S["ProfileSecretsInput"];
export type ConnectionTestResult = S["ConnectionTestResult"];
export type EngineInfo = S["EngineInfo"];
export type Engine = EngineInfo["engine"];
export type PathSegment = S["PathSegment"];
export type ObjectNode = S["ObjectNode"];
export type ObjectDetail = S["ObjectDetail"];
export type ColumnInfo = S["ColumnInfo"];
export type SessionInfo = S["SessionInfo"];
export type ColumnMeta = S["ColumnMeta"];
export type ServerEvent = S["ExecutionSchema"]["event"];
export type ClientCommand = S["ExecutionSchema"]["command"];

export function pathKey(path: PathSegment[]): string {
  return path.map((s) => `${s.kind}:${s.name}`).join("/");
}
```

```tsx
import { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

type Variant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger";
type Size = "sm" | "md";

const VARIANTS: Record<Variant, string> = {
  primary: "bg-accent text-accent-fg hover:brightness-110 disabled:opacity-50",
  secondary: "bg-elevated border border-border hover:bg-hover disabled:opacity-50",
  ghost: "hover:bg-hover disabled:opacity-40",
  danger: "bg-danger text-white hover:brightness-110 disabled:opacity-50",
};
const SIZES: Record<Size, string> = { sm: "h-6 px-2 text-[12px]", md: "h-7 px-3 text-[13px]" };

export interface ButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: Variant;
  size?: Size;
}

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(function Button(
  { variant = "secondary", size = "md", className, type = "button", ...props },
  ref,
) {
  return (
    <button
      ref={ref}
      type={type}
      className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md font-medium outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent/50", VARIANTS[variant], SIZES[size], className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
});
```

```tsx
import { forwardRef, type InputHTMLAttributes, type ReactNode, type SelectHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

const control = "h-7 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-elevated px-2 text-[13px] outline-none focus:border-accent";

export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>>(function Input(
  { className, ...props },
  ref,
) {
  return <input ref={ref} spellCheck={false} autoCorrect="off" autoCapitalize="off" className={cn(control, className)} {...props} />;
});

export function Select({ className, children, ...props }: SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement>) {
  return (
    <select className={cn(control, "pr-6", className)} {...props}>
      {children}
    </select>
  );
}

export function Checkbox({ label, ...props }: InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> & { label: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px]">
      <input type="checkbox" className="accent-[var(--accent)]" {...props} />
      {label}
    </label>
  );
}

export function FieldRow({ label, hint, children }: { label: string; hint?: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-[120px_1fr] items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1">
      <span className="text-right text-[12px] text-muted">{label}</span>
      <div>{children}</div>
      {hint && <span className="col-start-2 text-[11px] text-muted">{hint}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Dialog as RadixDialog } from "radix-ui";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

export function Dialog({
  open, onOpenChange, title, children, footer, width = 560,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  title: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  footer?: ReactNode;
  width?: number;
}) {
  return (
    <RadixDialog.Root open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <RadixDialog.Portal>
        <RadixDialog.Overlay className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/30" />
        <RadixDialog.Content
          style={{ width }}
          className="fixed left-1/2 top-[12%] max-h-[76vh] -translate-x-1/2 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-elevated shadow-2xl flex flex-col"
          onOpenAutoFocus={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
        >
          <RadixDialog.Title className="border-b border-border px-4 py-2.5 text-[13px] font-semibold">{title}</RadixDialog.Title>
          <RadixDialog.Description className="sr-only">{title}</RadixDialog.Description>
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto p-4">{children}</div>
          {footer && <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-border px-4 py-2.5">{footer}</div>}
        </RadixDialog.Content>
      </RadixDialog.Portal>
    </RadixDialog.Root>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

export function Tabs<T extends string>({
  value, onValueChange, items,
}: {
  value: T;
  onValueChange: (value: T) => void;
  items: { value: T; label: string }[];
}) {
  return (
    <div role="tablist" className="mb-3 flex gap-1 border-b border-border">
      {items.map((item) => (
        <button
          key={item.value}
          role="tab"
          aria-selected={value === item.value}
          onClick={() => onValueChange(item.value)}
          className={cn(
            "-mb-px border-b-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px]",
            value === item.value ? "border-accent text-fg" : "border-transparent text-muted hover:text-fg",
          )}
        >
          {item.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { create } from "zustand";
import { pathKey, type ObjectNode, type PathSegment } from "@/lib/api/types";

export interface ObjectTab {
  id: string;
  kind: "object";
  profileId: string;
  path: PathSegment[];
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
}
export interface ConsoleTab {
  id: string;
  kind: "console";
  profileId: string;
  database: string | null;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
}
export type Tab = ObjectTab | ConsoleTab;

interface WorkspaceState {
  tabs: Tab[];
  activeTabId: string | null;
  selectedProfileId: string | null;
  openObject: (profileId: string, node: ObjectNode, profileName: string) => void;
  openConsole: (profileId: string, database: string | null, profileName: string) => void;
  closeTab: (id: string) => void;
  activate: (id: string) => void;
  selectProfile: (id: string | null) => void;
}

let consoleCounter = 0;

export const useWorkspace = create<WorkspaceState>((set, get) => ({
  tabs: [],
  activeTabId: null,
  selectedProfileId: null,
  openObject: (profileId, node, profileName) => {
    const id = `object:${profileId}:${pathKey(node.path)}`;
    if (!get().tabs.some((t) => t.id === id)) {
      const tab: ObjectTab = { id, kind: "object", profileId, path: node.path, title: node.name, subtitle: profileName };
      set((s) => ({ tabs: [...s.tabs, tab] }));
    }
    set({ activeTabId: id });
  },
  openConsole: (profileId, database, profileName) => {
    consoleCounter += 1;
    const id = `console:${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
    const tab: ConsoleTab = { id, kind: "console", profileId, database, title: `Query ${consoleCounter}`, subtitle: profileName };
    set((s) => ({ tabs: [...s.tabs, tab], activeTabId: id }));
  },
  closeTab: (id) =>
    set((s) => {
      const index = s.tabs.findIndex((t) => t.id === id);
      const tabs = s.tabs.filter((t) => t.id !== id);
      const activeTabId = s.activeTabId === id ? (tabs[Math.min(index, tabs.length - 1)]?.id ?? null) : s.activeTabId;
      return { tabs, activeTabId };
    }),
  activate: (id) => set({ activeTabId: id }),
  selectProfile: (id) => set({ selectedProfileId: id }),
}));
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Connection hooks and folder tree**

`src/features/connections/hooks.ts`:
```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api, unwrap } from "@/lib/api/client";
import type { ProfileDraft, ProfileSecretsInput } from "@/lib/api/types";

export const profilesKey = ["profiles"] as const;

export function useProfiles() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: profilesKey, queryFn: async () => unwrap(await (await api()).GET("/profiles")) });
}

export function useEngines() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["engines"],
    queryFn: async () => unwrap(await (await api()).GET("/system/engines")),
    staleTime: Infinity,
  });
}

export function useSaveProfile() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { id?: string; profile: ProfileDraft; secrets: ProfileSecretsInput }) => {
      const client = await api();
      const body = { profile: input.profile, secrets: input.secrets };
      return input.id
        ? unwrap(await client.PUT("/profiles/{profile_id}", { params: { path: { profile_id: input.id } }, body }))
        : unwrap(await client.POST("/profiles", { body }));
    },
    onSuccess: (_, input) => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: profilesKey });
      if (input.id) {
        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["children", input.id] });
        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["detail", input.id] });
      }
    },
  });
}

export function useDeleteProfile() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: string) =>
      unwrap(await (await api()).DELETE("/profiles/{profile_id}", { params: { path: { profile_id: id } } })),
    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: profilesKey }),
  });
}

export function useTestConnection() {
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { profile: ProfileDraft; secrets: ProfileSecretsInput; existingId?: string }) =>
      unwrap(
        await (await api()).POST("/profiles/test", {
          body: { profile: input.profile, secrets: input.secrets, existing_id: input.existingId ?? null },
        }),
      ),
  });
}

export function useImportUrl() {
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (url: string) => unwrap(await (await api()).POST("/profiles/import-url", { body: { url } })),
  });
}

export function useUnlockProfile() {
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: { id: string; secrets: ProfileSecretsInput }) =>
      unwrap(
        await (await api()).POST("/profiles/{profile_id}/unlock", {
          params: { path: { profile_id: input.id } },
          body: input.secrets,
        }),
      ),
  });
}
```
(`unwrap` on a 204 returns `undefined`, which is fine for DELETE/unlock.)

`src/features/connections/folders.ts`:
```ts
import type { ProfileEntry } from "@/lib/api/types";

export interface FolderNode {
  name: string;
  path: string;
  folders: FolderNode[];
  profiles: ProfileEntry[];
}

/** Group profiles by their "/"-separated folder path. The root has name "" and path "". */
export function buildFolderTree(entries: ProfileEntry[]): FolderNode {
  const root: FolderNode = { name: "", path: "", folders: [], profiles: [] };
  for (const entry of entries) {
    const parts = entry.profile.folder.split("/").map((p) => p.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    let node = root;
    for (const part of parts) {
      const path = node.path ? `${node.path}/${part}` : part;
      let child = node.folders.find((f) => f.name === part);
      if (!child) {
        child = { name: part, path, folders: [], profiles: [] };
        node.folders.push(child);
      }
      node = child;
    }
    node.profiles.push(entry);
  }
  const sort = (node: FolderNode) => {
    node.folders.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    node.profiles.sort((a, b) => a.profile.name.localeCompare(b.profile.name));
    node.folders.forEach(sort);
  };
  sort(root);
  return root;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Connection dialog**

`src/features/connections/ConnectionDialog.tsx` — form for create/edit. Requirements:
- Engine picker shows engines from `useEngines()` (only supported ones: postgres, sqlite now) with labels `PostgreSQL`, `SQLite`, `MySQL`, `SQL Server`, `Couchbase`.
- Tabs: **General** (name, folder, color, engine-specific fields, password + "Save password in Keychain" checkbox, read-only checkbox), **SSL** (server engines: mode select, CA/cert/key file paths), **SSH** (enabled checkbox, host, port, user, auth select `agent_or_key`/`password`, identity file, SSH password), **Advanced** (key/value options as `key=value` lines in a textarea).
- "Import from URL" row at the top of General: input + "Import" → `useImportUrl` → replaces the draft fields and password.
- Password fields: in edit mode leave empty to keep the stored secret (placeholder "Saved in Keychain" when `entry.has_password`); send `null` when untouched, the new value when typed, `""` when the user clicks a "Clear" link.
- Footer: Test (shows ✓ message in green or ✗ message in red under the form), Cancel, Save (disabled while pending; shows `ApiError.message` on failure; closes on success).
- Port placeholder = engine `default_port`.

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Dialog } from "@/components/ui/Dialog";
import { Checkbox, FieldRow, Input, Select } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { Tabs } from "@/components/ui/Tabs";
import { ApiError } from "@/lib/api/client";
import type { ConnectionTestResult, Engine, ProfileDraft, ProfileEntry, ProfileSecretsInput, ServerProfileDraft } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { useEngines, useImportUrl, useSaveProfile, useTestConnection } from "./hooks";

export const ENGINE_LABELS: Record<Engine, string> = {
  postgres: "PostgreSQL", mysql: "MySQL", mssql: "SQL Server", couchbase: "Couchbase", sqlite: "SQLite",
};
const COLORS = ["none", "green", "yellow", "orange", "red", "purple"] as const;
type TabId = "general" | "ssl" | "ssh" | "advanced";

function emptyDraft(engine: Engine): ProfileDraft {
  if (engine === "sqlite") return { engine, name: "", path: "", folder: "", color: "none", read_only: false, save_password: true };
  return {
    engine, name: "", host: "127.0.0.1", port: null, database: null, username: null, folder: "", color: "none",
    read_only: false, save_password: true, options: {},
    ssl: { mode: "prefer", ca_file: null, cert_file: null, key_file: null },
    ssh: { enabled: false, host: "", port: null, user: null, identity_file: null, auth: "agent_or_key" },
  };
}

function toDraft(entry: ProfileEntry): ProfileDraft {
  const { id: _id, ...draft } = entry.profile;
  return draft as ProfileDraft;
}

function parseOptions(text: string): Record<string, string> {
  return Object.fromEntries(
    text.split("\n").map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l.includes("=")).map((l) => {
      const i = l.indexOf("=");
      return [l.slice(0, i).trim(), l.slice(i + 1).trim()];
    }),
  );
}

export function ConnectionDialog({
  open, onOpenChange, entry,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  entry?: ProfileEntry;
}) {
  const engines = useEngines();
  const save = useSaveProfile();
  const test = useTestConnection();
  const importUrl = useImportUrl();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<TabId>("general");
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ProfileDraft>(() => (entry ? toDraft(entry) : emptyDraft("postgres")));
  const [password, setPassword] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sshPassword, setSshPassword] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [optionsText, setOptionsText] = useState("");
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  const [testResult, setTestResult] = useState<ConnectionTestResult | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const initial = entry ? toDraft(entry) : emptyDraft("postgres");
    setDraft(initial);
    setPassword(null);
    setSshPassword(null);
    setOptionsText(initial.engine === "sqlite" ? "" : Object.entries(initial.options ?? {}).map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`).join("\n"));
    setTab("general");
    setTestResult(null);
    setError(null);
    setUrl("");
  }, [open, entry]);

  const engineInfo = engines.data?.find((e) => e.engine === draft.engine);
  const server = draft.engine === "sqlite" ? null : (draft as ServerProfileDraft);
  const patch = (changes: Partial<ProfileDraft>) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, ...changes }) as ProfileDraft);
  const patchServer = (changes: Partial<ServerProfileDraft>) => setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, ...changes }) as ProfileDraft);

  const finalDraft = useMemo<ProfileDraft>(
    () => (server ? { ...server, options: parseOptions(optionsText) } : draft),
    [draft, server, optionsText],
  );
  const secrets: ProfileSecretsInput = { password, ssh_password: sshPassword };

  const onSave = () => {
    setError(null);
    save.mutate(
      { id: entry?.profile.id, profile: finalDraft, secrets },
      { onSuccess: () => onOpenChange(false), onError: (e) => setError(e instanceof ApiError ? e.message : String(e)) },
    );
  };
  const onTest = () => {
    setTestResult(null);
    test.mutate(
      { profile: finalDraft, secrets, existingId: entry?.profile.id },
      { onSuccess: setTestResult, onError: (e) => setError(String(e)) },
    );
  };
  const onImport = () =>
    importUrl.mutate(url, {
      onSuccess: (result) => {
        setDraft({ ...emptyDraft(result.profile.engine), ...result.profile } as ProfileDraft);
        if (result.profile.engine !== "sqlite") {
          setOptionsText(Object.entries(result.profile.options ?? {}).map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`).join("\n"));
        }
        if (result.password) setPassword(result.password);
      },
      onError: (e) => setError(e instanceof ApiError ? e.message : String(e)),
    });

  const tabs = server
    ? [{ value: "general" as const, label: "General" }, { value: "ssl" as const, label: "SSL" },
       { value: "ssh" as const, label: "SSH" }, { value: "advanced" as const, label: "Advanced" }]
    : [{ value: "general" as const, label: "General" }];

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title={entry ? `Edit ${entry.profile.name}` : "New Connection"}
      footer={
        <>
          <Button onClick={onTest} disabled={test.isPending}>{test.isPending ? "Testing…" : "Test"}</Button>
          <div className="flex-1" />
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={onSave} disabled={save.isPending || !draft.name}>
            {save.isPending ? "Saving…" : "Save"}
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <Tabs value={tab} onValueChange={setTab} items={tabs} />
      {tab === "general" && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
          {!entry && (
            <FieldRow label="Import URL">
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <Input placeholder="postgres://user@host:5432/db or sqlite:///path/to.db" value={url} onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)} />
                <Button onClick={onImport} disabled={!url || importUrl.isPending}>Import</Button>
              </div>
            </FieldRow>
          )}
          <FieldRow label="Engine">
            <Select value={draft.engine} disabled={!!entry} onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...emptyDraft(e.target.value as Engine), name: draft.name, folder: draft.folder })}>
              {(engines.data ?? []).map((e) => <option key={e.engine} value={e.engine}>{ENGINE_LABELS[e.engine]}</option>)}
            </Select>
          </FieldRow>
          <FieldRow label="Name"><Input value={draft.name} onChange={(e) => patch({ name: e.target.value })} /></FieldRow>
          <FieldRow label="Folder" hint="Use / for nesting, e.g. Work/Prod"><Input value={draft.folder ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch({ folder: e.target.value })} /></FieldRow>
          <FieldRow label="Color">
            <Select value={draft.color ?? "none"} onChange={(e) => patch({ color: e.target.value as ProfileDraft["color"] })}>
              {COLORS.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </Select>
          </FieldRow>
          {server ? (
            <>
              <FieldRow label="Host">
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <Input value={server.host} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ host: e.target.value })} />
                  <Input className="w-24" placeholder={String(engineInfo?.default_port ?? "")} value={server.port ?? ""}
                    onChange={(e) => patchServer({ port: e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null })} />
                </div>
              </FieldRow>
              <FieldRow label="Database"><Input value={server.database ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ database: e.target.value || null })} /></FieldRow>
              <FieldRow label="User"><Input value={server.username ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ username: e.target.value || null })} /></FieldRow>
              <FieldRow label="Password">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Input type="password" placeholder={entry?.has_password ? "Saved in Keychain" : ""} value={password ?? ""} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
                  {entry?.has_password && <button className="text-[12px] text-accent" onClick={() => setPassword("")}>Clear</button>}
                </div>
              </FieldRow>
            </>
          ) : (
            <FieldRow label="File"><Input placeholder="/Users/me/app.db" value={draft.engine === "sqlite" ? draft.path : ""} onChange={(e) => patch({ path: e.target.value } as Partial<ProfileDraft>)} /></FieldRow>
          )}
          <FieldRow label="">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              {server && <Checkbox label="Save password in Keychain" checked={draft.save_password ?? true} onChange={(e) => patch({ save_password: e.target.checked })} />}
              <Checkbox label="Read-only connection" checked={draft.read_only ?? false} onChange={(e) => patch({ read_only: e.target.checked })} />
            </div>
          </FieldRow>
        </div>
      )}
      {tab === "ssl" && server && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
          <FieldRow label="Mode">
            <Select value={server.ssl?.mode ?? "prefer"} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ ssl: { ...server.ssl!, mode: e.target.value as NonNullable<ServerProfileDraft["ssl"]>["mode"] } })}>
              {["disable", "prefer", "require", "verify-ca", "verify-full"].map((m) => <option key={m} value={m}>{m}</option>)}
            </Select>
          </FieldRow>
          {(["ca_file", "cert_file", "key_file"] as const).map((field) => (
            <FieldRow key={field} label={{ ca_file: "CA file", cert_file: "Client cert", key_file: "Client key" }[field]}>
              <Input placeholder="/path/to/file.pem" value={server.ssl?.[field] ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ ssl: { ...server.ssl!, [field]: e.target.value || null } })} />
            </FieldRow>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {tab === "ssh" && server && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
          <FieldRow label=""><Checkbox label="Connect through an SSH tunnel" checked={server.ssh?.enabled ?? false} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ ssh: { ...server.ssh!, enabled: e.target.checked } })} /></FieldRow>
          <FieldRow label="SSH host" hint="A host or an alias from ~/.ssh/config">
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Input value={server.ssh?.host ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ ssh: { ...server.ssh!, host: e.target.value } })} />
              <Input className="w-24" placeholder="22" value={server.ssh?.port ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ ssh: { ...server.ssh!, port: e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null } })} />
            </div>
          </FieldRow>
          <FieldRow label="SSH user"><Input value={server.ssh?.user ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ ssh: { ...server.ssh!, user: e.target.value || null } })} /></FieldRow>
          <FieldRow label="Auth">
            <Select value={server.ssh?.auth ?? "agent_or_key"} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ ssh: { ...server.ssh!, auth: e.target.value as "agent_or_key" | "password" } })}>
              <option value="agent_or_key">SSH agent / key</option>
              <option value="password">Password</option>
            </Select>
          </FieldRow>
          {server.ssh?.auth === "password" ? (
            <FieldRow label="SSH password">
              <Input type="password" placeholder={entry?.has_ssh_password ? "Saved in Keychain" : ""} value={sshPassword ?? ""} onChange={(e) => setSshPassword(e.target.value)} />
            </FieldRow>
          ) : (
            <FieldRow label="Identity file" hint="Optional; the agent and ~/.ssh/config are used otherwise">
              <Input placeholder="~/.ssh/id_ed25519" value={server.ssh?.identity_file ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patchServer({ ssh: { ...server.ssh!, identity_file: e.target.value || null } })} />
            </FieldRow>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {tab === "advanced" && server && (
        <FieldRow label="Options" hint="One key=value per line, passed to the driver">
          <textarea className="h-32 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-elevated p-2 font-mono text-[12px] outline-none focus:border-accent" spellCheck={false} value={optionsText} onChange={(e) => setOptionsText(e.target.value)} />
        </FieldRow>
      )}
      {testResult && (
        <p className={testResult.ok ? "mt-3 text-[12px] text-success" : "mt-3 whitespace-pre-wrap text-[12px] text-danger"} data-selectable>
          {testResult.ok ? "✓" : "✗"} {testResult.message} ({testResult.elapsed_ms} ms)
        </p>
      )}
      {error && <p className="mt-3 whitespace-pre-wrap text-[12px] text-danger" data-selectable>{error}</p>}
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```
Fix any type mismatches the generated schema introduces (e.g. optional vs nullable fields) by adjusting the casts — keep the behaviour described above.

`src/features/connections/UnlockDialog.tsx` (asked when the backend answers `password_required`):
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Dialog } from "@/components/ui/Dialog";
import { FieldRow, Input } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import type { Profile } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { useUnlockProfile } from "./hooks";

export function UnlockDialog({
  profile, open, onOpenChange, onUnlocked,
}: {
  profile: Profile;
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  onUnlocked: () => void;
}) {
  const unlock = useUnlockProfile();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const submit = () =>
    unlock.mutate(
      { id: profile.id, secrets: { password, ssh_password: null } },
      { onSuccess: () => { setPassword(""); onOpenChange(false); onUnlocked(); } },
    );
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} title={`Password for ${profile.name}`} width={420}
      footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>Cancel</Button>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={submit} disabled={!password || unlock.isPending}>Connect</Button></>}>
      <FieldRow label="Password">
        <Input type="password" autoFocus value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && submit()} />
      </FieldRow>
      <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">Kept in memory until DbaGuy quits.</p>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`src/app/BackendBanner.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { listen } from "@tauri-apps/api/event";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { isTauriRuntime, restartBackend } from "@/lib/backend";

/** Shown when the sidecar exits unexpectedly (Tauri only). */
export function BackendBanner() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [exited, setExited] = useState(false);
  const [restarting, setRestarting] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isTauriRuntime()) return;
    const unlisten = listen("backend://exited", () => setExited(true));
    return () => {
      unlisten.then((fn) => fn());
    };
  }, []);
  if (!exited) return null;
  const restart = async () => {
    setRestarting(true);
    try {
      await restartBackend();
      setExited(false);
      await qc.invalidateQueries();
    } finally {
      setRestarting(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-border bg-danger/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px]">
      <span className="text-danger">The DbaGuy backend stopped unexpectedly. Your editor contents are kept.</span>
      <Button size="sm" onClick={restart} disabled={restarting}>{restarting ? "Restarting…" : "Restart backend"}</Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/app/AppShell.tsx` (react-resizable-panels: check the installed major's exports — v4 may name them `Group`/`Panel`/`Separator` instead of `PanelGroup`/`Panel`/`PanelResizeHandle`; use whatever the installed `.d.ts` exports):
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Panel, PanelGroup, PanelResizeHandle } from "react-resizable-panels";
import { api, unwrap } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { BackendBanner } from "./BackendBanner";

export function AppShell({ sidebar, workspace }: { sidebar: ReactNode; workspace: ReactNode }) {
  const health = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["health"],
    queryFn: async () => unwrap(await (await api()).GET("/system/health")),
    refetchInterval: 15_000,
  });
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div data-tauri-drag-region className="flex h-[38px] shrink-0 items-center border-b border-border bg-panel pl-[80px] pr-3">
        <span data-tauri-drag-region className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-muted">DbaGuy</span>
      </div>
      <BackendBanner />
      <PanelGroup direction="horizontal" className="min-h-0 flex-1" autoSaveId="dbaguy-main">
        <Panel defaultSize={22} minSize={15} className="bg-panel">{sidebar}</Panel>
        <PanelResizeHandle className="w-px bg-border data-[resize-handle-state=drag]:bg-accent" />
        <Panel minSize={40}>{workspace}</Panel>
      </PanelGroup>
      <div className="flex h-[22px] shrink-0 items-center gap-3 border-t border-border bg-panel px-3 text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span className={health.isError ? "text-danger" : ""}>
          {health.isError ? "Backend unreachable" : health.data ? `Backend v${health.data.version}` : "Connecting…"}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { AppShell } from "./app/AppShell";

export function App() {
  return (
    <AppShell
      sidebar={<div className="p-3 text-muted">Explorer (Task 13)</div>}
      workspace={<div className="p-3 text-muted">Workspace (Task 13)</div>}
    />
  );
}
```
