### Task 13: Explorer tree, object detail view, workspace tabs

**Files:**
- Create: `src/features/explorer/kinds.tsx`, `src/features/explorer/useTreeRows.ts`, `src/features/explorer/Explorer.tsx`, `src/features/explorer/ObjectDetailView.tsx`, `src/features/workspace/Workspace.tsx`, `src/components/ui/ContextMenu.tsx`, `src/components/ui/ConfirmDialog.tsx`
- Modify: `src/App.tsx` (use `Explorer` and `Workspace`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 12 hooks, primitives, `useWorkspace`, `pathKey`, `ConnectionDialog`, `UnlockDialog`, `buildFolderTree`; API `/metadata/children`, `/metadata/detail`.
- Produces:
  - `kindIcon(kind, folderName?) -> LucideIcon`, `folderLabel(name) -> string`, `kindLabel(kind) -> string`.
  - `useTreeRows(entries, expanded) -> TreeRow[]` where `TreeRow = {type:"folder", key, name, depth} | {type:"profile", key, entry, depth} | {type:"node", key, profileId, profileName, node, depth} | {type:"loading", key, depth} | {type:"error", key, depth, message, code, profileId, retry}`. Children are fetched with TanStack Query key `["children", profileId, pathKey(path)]`.
  - `Explorer` component (sidebar), `ObjectDetailView({tab})`, `Workspace` (tab bar + content). `Workspace` renders `QueryConsole` for console tabs once Task 14 exists; in this task render a placeholder for them.

Behaviour:
- Tree: folder rows → profile rows (color dot, engine label in muted text) → lazily loaded object nodes. Chevron toggles; a row with `has_children=false` has no chevron. Loading row shows "Loading…"; error row shows the message in red with "Retry"; `password_required` errors open `UnlockDialog` then retry.
- Selection: single selected row (highlight). Click selects; double-click or Enter on an object node opens it via `openObject`; on a profile row double-click toggles expansion.
- Keyboard (when the tree has focus): ↑/↓ move selection, → expand (or move to first child), ← collapse (or move to parent), Enter open, ⌘N new connection.
- Context menu on profile rows: "New Query" (`openConsole(profileId, null, name)`), "Edit…", "Refresh" (invalidate `["children", profileId]`), "Delete…" (ConfirmDialog, then `useDeleteProfile` and close that profile's tabs).
- Sidebar header: "Connections" + "+" button (opens `ConnectionDialog`) + "↻" refresh-all.
- Virtualized with `@tanstack/react-virtual` (row height 22 px, overscan 20) so schemas with thousands of objects stay fast.
- Object detail tab: header with kind label, name, "approximate" badge when `ddl_is_generated`, "Copy DDL" button (`navigator.clipboard.writeText`); columns table (Name, Type, Nullable, Default, PK, Comment) when `columns` is present; DDL in a `<pre data-selectable>` with `font-mono text-[12px]`. Loading and error states shown inline.
- Workspace: horizontal tab bar (title + muted subtitle = profile name, close ×, middle-click closes, ⌘W closes the active tab), content area below. Empty state text: "Open a table from the sidebar, or right-click a connection → New Query".

```tsx
import {
  Braces, Columns3, Database, Eye, Folder, FunctionSquare, Hash, KeyRound, Layers, ListTree,
  Shapes, Split, Table2, Workflow, Zap, type LucideIcon,
} from "lucide-react";

const KIND_ICONS: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  database: Database, schema: Braces, table: Table2, partition: Split, view: Eye, materialized_view: Layers,
  function: FunctionSquare, procedure: Workflow, sequence: Hash, type: Shapes, index: KeyRound,
  trigger: Zap, column: Columns3,
};
const FOLDER_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  tables: "Tables", views: "Views", materialized_views: "Materialized Views", functions: "Functions",
  procedures: "Procedures", sequences: "Sequences", types: "Types", columns: "Columns", indexes: "Indexes",
  triggers: "Triggers", partitions: "Partitions",
};
const KIND_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  database: "Database", schema: "Schema", table: "Table", partition: "Partition", view: "View",
  materialized_view: "Materialized View", function: "Function", procedure: "Procedure", sequence: "Sequence",
  type: "Type", index: "Index", trigger: "Trigger", column: "Column",
};

export function kindIcon(kind: string): LucideIcon {
  return kind === "folder" ? Folder : (KIND_ICONS[kind] ?? ListTree);
}
export function folderLabel(name: string): string {
  return FOLDER_LABELS[name] ?? name;
}
export function kindLabel(kind: string): string {
  return KIND_LABELS[kind] ?? kind;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Tree rows hook**

`src/features/explorer/useTreeRows.ts`:
```ts
import { useQueries } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { api, ApiError, unwrap } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { pathKey, type ObjectNode, type PathSegment, type ProfileEntry } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { buildFolderTree, type FolderNode } from "../connections/folders";

export type TreeRow =
  | { type: "folder"; key: string; name: string; depth: number }
  | { type: "profile"; key: string; entry: ProfileEntry; depth: number }
  | { type: "node"; key: string; profileId: string; profileName: string; node: ObjectNode; depth: number }
  | { type: "loading"; key: string; depth: number }
  | { type: "error"; key: string; depth: number; message: string; code: string; profileId: string; retry: () => void };

export const folderKey = (path: string) => `folder:${path}`;
export const profileKey = (profileId: string) => `profile:${profileId}`;
export const nodeKey = (profileId: string, path: PathSegment[]) => `node:${profileId}:${pathKey(path)}`;

interface Expansion {
  profileId: string;
  path: PathSegment[];
}

/** Flattens folders → profiles → lazily loaded objects into visible rows. */
export function useTreeRows(entries: ProfileEntry[], expanded: ReadonlySet<string>, expansions: Map<string, Expansion>) {
  const targets = useMemo(() => [...expansions.entries()].filter(([key]) => expanded.has(key)), [expansions, expanded]);
  const results = useQueries({
    queries: targets.map(([, target]) => ({
      queryKey: ["children", target.profileId, pathKey(target.path)],
      queryFn: async () =>
        unwrap(await (await api()).POST("/metadata/children", { body: { profile_id: target.profileId, path: target.path } })),
      staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
    })),
  });

  return useMemo(() => {
    const byKey = new Map(targets.map(([key], i) => [key, results[i]]));
    const rows: TreeRow[] = [];
    const pushChildren = (profileId: string, profileName: string, ownerKey: string, depth: number) => {
      const result = byKey.get(ownerKey);
      if (!result || result.isPending) {
        rows.push({ type: "loading", key: `${ownerKey}#loading`, depth });
        return;
      }
      if (result.isError) {
        const err = result.error;
        rows.push({
          type: "error", key: `${ownerKey}#error`, depth, profileId,
          message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
          code: err instanceof ApiError ? err.code : "error",
          retry: () => void result.refetch(),
        });
        return;
      }
      for (const node of result.data ?? []) {
        const key = nodeKey(profileId, node.path);
        rows.push({ type: "node", key, profileId, profileName, node, depth });
        if (node.has_children && expanded.has(key)) pushChildren(profileId, profileName, key, depth + 1);
      }
    };
    const walk = (folder: FolderNode, depth: number) => {
      for (const sub of folder.folders) {
        const key = folderKey(sub.path);
        rows.push({ type: "folder", key, name: sub.name, depth });
        if (expanded.has(key)) walk(sub, depth + 1);
      }
      for (const entry of folder.profiles) {
        const key = profileKey(entry.profile.id);
        rows.push({ type: "profile", key, entry, depth });
        if (expanded.has(key)) pushChildren(entry.profile.id, entry.profile.name, key, depth + 1);
      }
    };
    walk(buildFolderTree(entries), 0);
    return rows;
  }, [entries, expanded, targets, results]);
}
```
`expansions` maps an expandable row key to the `{profileId, path}` whose children it shows: for a profile row `{profileId, path: []}`, for a node row `{profileId, path: node.path}`. The `Explorer` adds an entry when a row is first expanded.

```tsx
import { ContextMenu as Radix } from "radix-ui";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

export interface MenuItem {
  label: string;
  onSelect: () => void;
  danger?: boolean;
  separatorBefore?: boolean;
}

export function ContextMenu({ items, children }: { items: MenuItem[]; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <Radix.Root>
      <Radix.Trigger asChild>{children}</Radix.Trigger>
      <Radix.Portal>
        <Radix.Content className="min-w-[180px] rounded-lg border border-border bg-elevated p-1 shadow-xl">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div key={item.label}>
              {item.separatorBefore && <Radix.Separator className="my-1 h-px bg-border" />}
              <Radix.Item
                onSelect={item.onSelect}
                className={`cursor-default rounded px-2 py-1 text-[13px] outline-none data-[highlighted]:bg-accent data-[highlighted]:text-accent-fg ${item.danger ? "text-danger" : ""}`}
              >
                {item.label}
              </Radix.Item>
            </div>
          ))}
        </Radix.Content>
      </Radix.Portal>
    </Radix.Root>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Explorer**

`src/features/explorer/Explorer.tsx` — implement the behaviour listed above. Core structure:
```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useVirtualizer } from "@tanstack/react-virtual";
import { ChevronRight, Plus, RefreshCw, Server } from "lucide-react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { ConfirmDialog } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmDialog";
import { ContextMenu } from "@/components/ui/ContextMenu";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import type { PathSegment, Profile, ProfileEntry } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { useWorkspace } from "@/stores/workspace";
import { ConnectionDialog, ENGINE_LABELS } from "../connections/ConnectionDialog";
import { useDeleteProfile, useProfiles } from "../connections/hooks";
import { UnlockDialog } from "../connections/UnlockDialog";
import { folderLabel, kindIcon } from "./kinds";
import { nodeKey, profileKey, useTreeRows, type TreeRow } from "./useTreeRows";

const ROW_HEIGHT = 22;
const COLOR_CLASSES: Record<string, string> = {
  none: "bg-transparent", green: "bg-green-500", yellow: "bg-yellow-400", orange: "bg-orange-500",
  red: "bg-red-500", purple: "bg-purple-500",
};

export function Explorer() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const profiles = useProfiles();
  const deleteProfile = useDeleteProfile();
  const { openObject, openConsole, closeTab, tabs } = useWorkspace();
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [expansions] = useState(() => new Map<string, { profileId: string; path: PathSegment[] }>());
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<{ open: boolean; entry?: ProfileEntry }>({ open: false });
  const [toDelete, setToDelete] = useState<ProfileEntry | null>(null);
  const [unlock, setUnlock] = useState<{ profile: Profile; retry: () => void } | null>(null);
  const entries = profiles.data ?? [];
  const rows = useTreeRows(entries, expanded, expansions);
  const parentRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const virtualizer = useVirtualizer({ count: rows.length, getScrollElement: () => parentRef.current, estimateSize: () => ROW_HEIGHT, overscan: 20 });

  const isExpandable = (row: TreeRow) =>
    row.type === "folder" || row.type === "profile" || (row.type === "node" && row.node.has_children);

  const toggle = useCallback((row: TreeRow, force?: boolean) => {
    if (row.type === "profile") expansions.set(row.key, { profileId: row.entry.profile.id, path: [] });
    if (row.type === "node") expansions.set(row.key, { profileId: row.profileId, path: row.node.path });
    setExpanded((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      const open = force ?? !next.has(row.key);
      if (open) next.add(row.key);
      else next.delete(row.key);
      return next;
    });
  }, [expansions]);

  const activate = (row: TreeRow) => {
    if (row.type === "node") {
      if (row.node.kind === "folder" || row.node.kind === "database" || row.node.kind === "schema") toggle(row);
      else openObject(row.profileId, row.node, row.profileName);
    } else if (isExpandable(row)) toggle(row);
  };

  // password_required → ask once, then retry
  useEffect(() => {
    const row = rows.find((r) => r.type === "error" && r.code === "password_required");
    if (row && row.type === "error" && !unlock) {
      const entry = entries.find((e) => e.profile.id === row.profileId);
      if (entry) setUnlock({ profile: entry.profile, retry: row.retry });
    }
  }, [rows, entries, unlock]);

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    const index = rows.findIndex((r) => r.key === selected);
    const row = rows[index];
    const move = (i: number) => {
      const target = rows[Math.max(0, Math.min(rows.length - 1, i))];
      if (target) { setSelected(target.key); virtualizer.scrollToIndex(rows.indexOf(target)); }
    };
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown") { e.preventDefault(); move(index + 1); }
    else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") { e.preventDefault(); move(index - 1); }
    else if (e.key === "ArrowRight" && row) {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (isExpandable(row) && !expanded.has(row.key)) toggle(row, true); else move(index + 1);
    } else if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" && row) {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (expanded.has(row.key)) toggle(row, false);
      else { for (let i = index - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (rows[i].depth < row.depth) { move(i); break; } }
    } else if (e.key === "Enter" && row) { e.preventDefault(); activate(row); }
    else if (e.key === "n" && e.metaKey) { e.preventDefault(); setDialog({ open: true }); }
  };

  const renderRow = (row: TreeRow) => {
    const pad = { paddingLeft: 8 + row.depth * 14 };
    const chevron = isExpandable(row) ? (
      <ChevronRight size={12} className={cn("shrink-0 text-muted transition-transform", expanded.has(row.key) && "rotate-90")}
        onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggle(row); }} />
    ) : <span className="w-3 shrink-0" />;
    const base = cn("flex h-[22px] items-center gap-1.5 pr-2 text-[13px]", selected === row.key ? "bg-selected" : "hover:bg-hover");
    switch (row.type) {
      case "folder": {
        const Icon = kindIcon("folder");
        return <div className={base} style={pad} onClick={() => setSelected(row.key)} onDoubleClick={() => toggle(row)}>{chevron}<Icon size={14} className="text-muted" /><span className="truncate">{row.name}</span></div>;
      }
      case "profile": {
        const p = row.entry.profile;
        const content = (
          <div className={base} style={pad} onClick={() => setSelected(row.key)} onDoubleClick={() => toggle(row)}>
            {chevron}<Server size={14} className="text-muted" />
            <span className={cn("h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full", COLOR_CLASSES[p.color ?? "none"])} />
            <span className="truncate font-medium">{p.name}</span>
            <span className="ml-auto shrink-0 text-[11px] text-muted">{ENGINE_LABELS[p.engine]}</span>
          </div>
        );
        return (
          <ContextMenu items={[
            { label: "New Query", onSelect: () => openConsole(p.id, null, p.name) },
            { label: "Edit…", onSelect: () => setDialog({ open: true, entry: row.entry }) },
            { label: "Refresh", onSelect: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["children", p.id] }) },
            { label: "Delete…", onSelect: () => setToDelete(row.entry), danger: true, separatorBefore: true },
          ]}>{content}</ContextMenu>
        );
      }
      case "node": {
        const Icon = kindIcon(row.node.kind);
        const label = row.node.kind === "folder" ? folderLabel(row.node.name) : row.node.name;
        return (
          <div className={cn(base, row.node.system && "opacity-60")} style={pad} title={row.node.detail ?? undefined}
            onClick={() => setSelected(row.key)} onDoubleClick={() => activate(row)}>
            {chevron}<Icon size={14} className="shrink-0 text-muted" />
            <span className="truncate">{label}</span>
            {row.node.detail && <span className="ml-auto shrink-0 truncate pl-2 text-[11px] text-muted">{row.node.detail}</span>}
          </div>
        );
      }
      case "loading":
        return <div className={base} style={pad}><span className="w-3" /><span className="text-muted">Loading…</span></div>;
      case "error":
        return (
          <div className={base} style={pad} title={row.message}>
            <span className="w-3" /><span className="truncate text-danger">{row.message}</span>
            <button className="ml-auto shrink-0 text-[11px] text-accent" onClick={row.retry}>Retry</button>
          </div>
        );
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className="flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 border-b border-border px-2">
        <span className="flex-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted">Connections</span>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" title="Refresh" onClick={() => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["children"] })}><RefreshCw size={13} /></Button>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" title="New connection (⌘N)" onClick={() => setDialog({ open: true })}><Plus size={14} /></Button>
      </div>
      <div ref={parentRef} tabIndex={0} onKeyDown={onKeyDown} className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto outline-none">
        {profiles.isError && <p className="p-3 text-[12px] text-danger">{String(profiles.error)}</p>}
        {profiles.data?.length === 0 && (
          <div className="p-4 text-[12px] text-muted">No connections yet. <button className="text-accent" onClick={() => setDialog({ open: true })}>Create one</button></div>
        )}
        <div style={{ height: virtualizer.getTotalSize(), position: "relative" }}>
          {virtualizer.getVirtualItems().map((item) => (
            <div key={rows[item.index].key} style={{ position: "absolute", top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, transform: `translateY(${item.start}px)` }}>
              {renderRow(rows[item.index])}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <ConnectionDialog open={dialog.open} entry={dialog.entry} onOpenChange={(open) => setDialog((d) => ({ ...d, open }))} />
      {toDelete && (
        <ConfirmDialog open title="Delete connection" pending={deleteProfile.isPending}
          message={`Delete "${toDelete.profile.name}"? Its saved password is removed from the Keychain.`}
          onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setToDelete(null)}
          onConfirm={() => deleteProfile.mutate(toDelete.profile.id, {
            onSuccess: () => {
              tabs.filter((t) => t.profileId === toDelete.profile.id).forEach((t) => closeTab(t.id));
              setToDelete(null);
            },
          })} />
      )}
      {unlock && (
        <UnlockDialog open profile={unlock.profile} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setUnlock(null)}
          onUnlocked={() => { unlock.retry(); setUnlock(null); }} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export { nodeKey, profileKey };
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Object detail view and workspace**

`src/features/explorer/ObjectDetailView.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Copy } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { api, unwrap } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { pathKey } from "@/lib/api/types";
import type { ObjectTab } from "@/stores/workspace";
import { kindLabel } from "./kinds";

export function ObjectDetailView({ tab }: { tab: ObjectTab }) {
  const detail = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["detail", tab.profileId, pathKey(tab.path)],
    queryFn: async () => unwrap(await (await api()).POST("/metadata/detail", { body: { profile_id: tab.profileId, path: tab.path } })),
  });
  if (detail.isPending) return <p className="p-4 text-muted">Loading…</p>;
  if (detail.isError) return <p className="p-4 whitespace-pre-wrap text-danger" data-selectable>{String(detail.error)}</p>;
  const d = detail.data;
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col overflow-auto">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-border px-4 py-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-muted">{kindLabel(d.kind)}</span>
        <span className="font-semibold" data-selectable>{d.name}</span>
        {d.ddl_is_generated && <span className="rounded bg-hover px-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted" title="Rebuilt from the catalog; may differ from the original statement">approximate</span>}
        <div className="flex-1" />
        {d.ddl && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(d.ddl!)}><Copy size={12} /> Copy DDL</Button>}
      </div>
      {d.comment && <p className="px-4 pt-2 text-[12px] text-muted" data-selectable>{d.comment}</p>}
      {d.columns && (
        <table className="mx-4 mt-3 border-collapse text-[12px]" data-selectable>
          <thead>
            <tr className="text-left text-muted">
              {["Name", "Type", "Nullable", "Default", "PK", "Comment"].map((h) => <th key={h} className="border-b border-border px-2 py-1 font-medium">{h}</th>)}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {d.columns.map((c) => (
              <tr key={c.name} className="hover:bg-hover">
                <td className="px-2 py-0.5 font-medium">{c.name}</td>
                <td className="px-2 py-0.5 font-mono">{c.type_name}</td>
                <td className="px-2 py-0.5">{c.nullable ? "yes" : "no"}</td>
                <td className="px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-muted">{c.default ?? ""}</td>
                <td className="px-2 py-0.5">{c.is_primary_key ? "●" : ""}</td>
                <td className="px-2 py-0.5 text-muted">{c.comment ?? ""}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      )}
      {d.ddl && <pre className="m-4 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-md border border-border bg-panel p-3 font-mono text-[12px]" data-selectable>{d.ddl}</pre>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/features/workspace/Workspace.tsx`:
```tsx
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { useWorkspace } from "@/stores/workspace";
import { ObjectDetailView } from "../explorer/ObjectDetailView";

export function Workspace() {
  const { tabs, activeTabId, activate, closeTab } = useWorkspace();
  const active = tabs.find((t) => t.id === activeTabId) ?? null;

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.metaKey && e.key === "w" && activeTabId) {
        e.preventDefault();
        closeTab(activeTabId);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [activeTabId, closeTab]);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className="flex h-8 shrink-0 items-end gap-px overflow-x-auto border-b border-border bg-panel px-1">
        {tabs.map((tab) => (
          <div key={tab.id} onClick={() => activate(tab.id)} onAuxClick={(e) => e.button === 1 && closeTab(tab.id)}
            className={cn("group flex h-7 max-w-[220px] shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-t-md px-2.5 text-[12px]",
              tab.id === activeTabId ? "bg-bg" : "text-muted hover:bg-hover")}>
            <span className="truncate">{tab.title}</span>
            <span className="truncate text-[11px] text-muted">{tab.subtitle}</span>
            <button className="rounded opacity-0 hover:bg-hover group-hover:opacity-100" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); closeTab(tab.id); }}><X size={12} /></button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        {!active && <p className="p-6 text-center text-muted">Open a table from the sidebar, or right-click a connection → New Query</p>}
        {tabs.map((tab) => (
          <div key={tab.id} className={cn("h-full", tab.id !== activeTabId && "hidden")}>
            {tab.kind === "object" ? <ObjectDetailView tab={tab} /> : <p className="p-4 text-muted">Console (Task 14)</p>}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```
(Tabs stay mounted while hidden so console sessions keep their state.)

### Task 14: Minimal query console over the session WebSocket

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/cells.ts`, `src/lib/api/session-socket.ts`, `src/features/console/execution.ts`, `src/features/console/useConsoleSession.ts`, `src/features/console/ResultTable.tsx`, `src/features/console/QueryConsole.tsx`
- Modify: `src/features/workspace/Workspace.tsx` (render `QueryConsole` for console tabs)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: WS protocol (Task 11), `ServerEvent`, `ClientCommand`, `ColumnMeta`, `SessionInfo` (Task 12 types), `getBackendInfo`, `api`, `unwrap`, `ApiError`, `UnlockDialog`, `useProfiles`, `ConsoleTab`.
- Produces (reused by sub-project ②):
  - `formatCell(value) -> {text, kind: "null"|"bytes"|"truncated"|"plain"}`.
  - `SessionSocket(sessionId)` with `connect()`, `send(command)`, `subscribe(listener) -> unsubscribe`, `onStatus(listener) -> unsubscribe`, `close()`; reconnects with backoff (250 ms … 5 s) except after close codes 4401/4404 or `close()`.
  - `ExecutionState`, `ResultSetState`, `newExecution(requestId)`, `applyEvent(state, event) -> ExecutionState`.
  - `useConsoleSession(profileId, database) -> {session, socket, status, error, retry}` (creates the session on mount, deletes it on unmount; handles StrictMode double mounts).
  - `QueryConsole({tab})`.

This console is deliberately minimal (a textarea); sub-project ② replaces the editor and the table. It exists to prove the pipeline: paging, load more, cancel, messages, errors with position, transaction state.

- [ ] **Step 1: Cell formatting and the socket**

`src/lib/cells.ts`:
```ts
export type CellKind = "null" | "bytes" | "truncated" | "plain";

/** Display text for an encoded cell (see backend adapters/values.py). */
export function formatCell(value: unknown): { text: string; kind: CellKind } {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return { text: "NULL", kind: "null" };
  if (typeof value === "object") {
    const v = value as { $type?: string; preview?: string; size?: number };
    if (v.$type === "bytes") return { text: `0x${v.preview ?? ""}${(v.size ?? 0) > 64 ? "…" : ""}`, kind: "bytes" };
    if (v.$type === "text") return { text: `${v.preview ?? ""}…`, kind: "truncated" };
    return { text: JSON.stringify(value), kind: "plain" };
  }
  return { text: String(value), kind: "plain" };
}
```

`src/lib/api/session-socket.ts`:
```ts
import { getBackendInfo } from "../backend";
import type { ClientCommand, ServerEvent } from "./types";

export type SocketStatus = "connecting" | "open" | "closed";
type Listener = (event: ServerEvent) => void;
type StatusListener = (status: SocketStatus) => void;

/** One WebSocket per session; queues commands until open and reconnects with backoff. */
export class SessionSocket {
  private ws: WebSocket | null = null;
  private readonly listeners = new Set<Listener>();
  private readonly statusListeners = new Set<StatusListener>();
  private readonly pending: ClientCommand[] = [];
  private closedByUser = false;
  private attempts = 0;
  private timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

  constructor(private readonly sessionId: string) {}

  async connect(): Promise<void> {
    const { baseUrl, token } = await getBackendInfo();
    if (this.closedByUser) return;
    const url = `${baseUrl.replace(/^http/, "ws")}/sessions/${this.sessionId}/ws?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`;
    this.setStatus("connecting");
    const ws = new WebSocket(url);
    this.ws = ws;
    ws.onopen = () => {
      this.attempts = 0;
      this.setStatus("open");
      for (const command of this.pending.splice(0)) ws.send(JSON.stringify(command));
    };
    ws.onmessage = (message) => {
      const event = JSON.parse(message.data as string) as ServerEvent;
      this.listeners.forEach((listener) => listener(event));
    };
    ws.onclose = (close) => {
      this.ws = null;
      this.setStatus("closed");
      if (this.closedByUser || close.code === 4401 || close.code === 4404) return;
      const delay = Math.min(5000, 250 * 2 ** this.attempts++);
      this.timer = setTimeout(() => void this.connect(), delay);
    };
  }

  send(command: ClientCommand): void {
    if (this.ws?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) this.ws.send(JSON.stringify(command));
    else this.pending.push(command);
  }

  subscribe(listener: Listener): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  }

  onStatus(listener: StatusListener): () => void {
    this.statusListeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.statusListeners.delete(listener);
  }

  close(): void {
    this.closedByUser = true;
    if (this.timer) clearTimeout(this.timer);
    this.ws?.close();
  }

  private setStatus(status: SocketStatus): void {
    this.statusListeners.forEach((listener) => listener(status));
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Execution reducer**

`src/features/console/execution.ts`:
```ts
import type { ColumnMeta, ServerEvent } from "@/lib/api/types";

export interface ResultSetState {
  index: number;
  columns: ColumnMeta[];
  rows: unknown[][];
  rowCount: number;
  hasMore: boolean;
}

export interface ExecutionState {
  requestId: string;
  status: "running" | "paused" | "done";
  resultSets: ResultSetState[];
  affected: { index: number; rows: number }[];
  messages: { severity: string; text: string }[];
  error: { message: string; code?: string | null; position?: { line: number; column: number } | null } | null;
  elapsedMs: number | null;
  inTransaction: boolean;
  cancelled: boolean;
}

export function newExecution(requestId: string): ExecutionState {
  return {
    requestId, status: "running", resultSets: [], affected: [], messages: [], error: null,
    elapsedMs: null, inTransaction: false, cancelled: false,
  };
}

function updateSet(state: ExecutionState, index: number, fn: (set: ResultSetState) => ResultSetState): ExecutionState {
  return { ...state, resultSets: state.resultSets.map((s) => (s.index === index ? fn(s) : s)) };
}

export function applyEvent(state: ExecutionState, event: ServerEvent): ExecutionState {
  switch (event.type) {
    case "started":
      return { ...state, status: "running" };
    case "result_set":
      return {
        ...state,
        resultSets: [...state.resultSets, { index: event.index, columns: event.columns, rows: [], rowCount: 0, hasMore: false }],
      };
    case "rows":
      return updateSet(state, event.index, (s) => ({ ...s, rows: s.rows.concat(event.rows) }));
    case "result_set_end": {
      const next = updateSet(state, event.index, (s) => ({ ...s, rowCount: event.row_count, hasMore: event.has_more }));
      return event.has_more ? { ...next, status: "paused" } : next;
    }
    case "affected":
      return { ...state, affected: [...state.affected, { index: event.index, rows: event.rows }] };
    case "message":
      return { ...state, messages: [...state.messages, { severity: event.severity, text: event.text }] };
    case "error":
      return { ...state, error: { message: event.message, code: event.code, position: event.position } };
    case "finished":
      return {
        ...state, status: "done", elapsedMs: event.elapsed_ms, inTransaction: event.in_transaction,
        cancelled: event.cancelled ?? false,
        resultSets: state.resultSets.map((s) => ({ ...s, hasMore: false })),
      };
    default:
      return state;
  }
}
```
If the generated `ServerEvent` union does not narrow on `type` (e.g. `type` is optional because it has a default), make the backend fields required in the schema instead of casting: in `domain/execution.py` the `type` fields already have defaults — add `json_schema_extra` or keep `separate_input_output_schemas=False` and, if needed, annotate the TS side with a local `type Narrow<T> = T & { type: NonNullable<T["type"]> }`. The reducer must type-check without `any`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Session lifecycle hook**

`src/features/console/useConsoleSession.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api, unwrap } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { SessionSocket, type SocketStatus } from "@/lib/api/session-socket";
import type { SessionInfo } from "@/lib/api/types";

interface ConsoleSession {
  session: SessionInfo | null;
  socket: SessionSocket | null;
  status: SocketStatus;
  error: Error | null;
}

export function useConsoleSession(profileId: string, database: string | null) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<ConsoleSession>({ session: null, socket: null, status: "connecting", error: null });
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    let socket: SessionSocket | null = null;
    let sessionId: string | null = null;
    const dispose = (id: string) =>
      void api().then((c) => c.DELETE("/sessions/{session_id}", { params: { path: { session_id: id } } }));
    setState({ session: null, socket: null, status: "connecting", error: null });
    (async () => {
      try {
        const session = unwrap(await (await api()).POST("/sessions", { body: { profile_id: profileId, database } }));
        sessionId = session.session_id;
        if (cancelled) {
          dispose(sessionId); // StrictMode / fast unmount: do not leak the session
          return;
        }
        socket = new SessionSocket(sessionId);
        socket.onStatus((status) => !cancelled && setState((s) => ({ ...s, status })));
        setState({ session, socket, status: "connecting", error: null });
        await socket.connect();
      } catch (error) {
        if (!cancelled) setState((s) => ({ ...s, error: error as Error }));
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      socket?.close();
      if (sessionId) dispose(sessionId);
    };
  }, [profileId, database, attempt]);

  return { ...state, retry: () => setAttempt((a) => a + 1) };
}
```

```tsx
import { formatCell } from "@/lib/cells";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import type { ResultSetState } from "./execution";

export function ResultTable({ set }: { set: ResultSetState }) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-auto rounded-md border border-border" data-selectable>
      <table className="border-collapse font-mono text-[12px]">
        <thead className="sticky top-0 bg-panel">
          <tr>
            <th className="border-b border-r border-border px-2 py-1 text-right text-muted">#</th>
            {set.columns.map((c) => (
              <th key={c.name} className="border-b border-r border-border px-2 py-1 text-left font-semibold" title={c.type_name}>
                {c.name}
                <span className="ml-1 font-normal text-muted">{c.type_name}</span>
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {set.rows.map((row, r) => (
            <tr key={r} className="hover:bg-hover">
              <td className="border-r border-border px-2 text-right text-muted">{r + 1}</td>
              {row.map((value, c) => {
                const cell = formatCell(value);
                return (
                  <td key={c} className={cn("max-w-[420px] truncate border-r border-border px-2",
                    set.columns[c]?.category === "number" && "text-right",
                    cell.kind === "null" && "italic text-muted", cell.kind === "bytes" && "text-muted")}>
                    {cell.text}
                  </td>
                );
              })}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/features/console/QueryConsole.tsx` — requirements:
- Toolbar: Run (⌘↵; runs the selection if any, else the whole text), Cancel (enabled while `status === "running"`), connection badge (`session.server.version` · database), socket status dot (green open / yellow connecting / red closed).
- Editor: `<textarea>` mono 13 px, `spellCheck={false}`; Tab inserts two spaces; ⌘↵ runs.
- Results (below the editor, vertical resizable split): error box (message, `Line L, column C` when present, offset by the selection start line when a selection was run), each result set as `ResultTable` with a footer "N rows" / "N rows loaded — Load 500 more" button when `hasMore`, affected rows lines ("3 rows affected"), messages list (warnings in yellow), summary line (elapsed ms, "Cancelled", "In transaction" badge in orange when `inTransaction`).
- Session errors: `password_required` → `UnlockDialog` then `retry()`; other errors → red text + Retry button.

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";
import { Panel, PanelGroup, PanelResizeHandle } from "react-resizable-panels";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { ApiError } from "@/lib/api/client";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import type { ConsoleTab } from "@/stores/workspace";
import { useProfiles } from "../connections/hooks";
import { UnlockDialog } from "../connections/UnlockDialog";
import { applyEvent, newExecution, type ExecutionState } from "./execution";
import { ResultTable } from "./ResultTable";
import { useConsoleSession } from "./useConsoleSession";

const PAGE = 500;

export function QueryConsole({ tab }: { tab: ConsoleTab }) {
  const { session, socket, status, error, retry } = useConsoleSession(tab.profileId, tab.database);
  const profiles = useProfiles();
  const [sql, setSql] = useState("");
  const [execution, setExecution] = useState<ExecutionState | null>(null);
  const [lineOffset, setLineOffset] = useState(0);
  const editor = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!socket) return;
    return socket.subscribe((event) =>
      setExecution((prev) => (prev && event.request_id === prev.requestId ? applyEvent(prev, event) : prev)),
    );
  }, [socket]);

  const run = () => {
    if (!socket || execution?.status === "running") return;
    const el = editor.current;
    const hasSelection = !!el && el.selectionEnd > el.selectionStart;
    const text = hasSelection ? sql.slice(el!.selectionStart, el!.selectionEnd) : sql;
    if (!text.trim()) return;
    setLineOffset(hasSelection ? sql.slice(0, el!.selectionStart).split("\n").length - 1 : 0);
    const requestId = crypto.randomUUID();
    setExecution(newExecution(requestId));
    socket.send({ type: "execute", request_id: requestId, sql: text, page_size: PAGE });
  };
  const cancel = () => execution && socket?.send({ type: "cancel", request_id: execution.requestId });
  const loadMore = () => {
    if (!execution || !socket) return;
    setExecution({ ...execution, status: "running" });
    socket.send({ type: "fetch_more", request_id: execution.requestId, count: PAGE });
  };
  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    if (e.key === "Enter" && e.metaKey) { e.preventDefault(); run(); }
    else if (e.key === "Tab") {
      e.preventDefault();
      const el = e.currentTarget;
      const { selectionStart: s, selectionEnd: end } = el;
      setSql(sql.slice(0, s) + "  " + sql.slice(end));
      requestAnimationFrame(() => el.setSelectionRange(s + 2, s + 2));
    }
  };

  const profile = profiles.data?.find((p) => p.profile.id === tab.profileId)?.profile;
  const needsPassword = error instanceof ApiError && error.code === "password_required";
  const dot = status === "open" ? "bg-success" : status === "connecting" ? "bg-yellow-400" : "bg-danger";

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className="flex h-9 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-border px-2">
        <Button variant="primary" size="sm" onClick={run} disabled={!socket || execution?.status === "running"}>Run ⌘↵</Button>
        <Button size="sm" onClick={cancel} disabled={execution?.status !== "running"}>Cancel</Button>
        <div className="flex-1" />
        <span className={cn("h-2 w-2 rounded-full", dot)} title={status} />
        <span className="truncate text-[11px] text-muted">
          {session ? `${session.server.version}${session.database ? ` · ${session.database}` : ""}` : "Connecting…"}
        </span>
      </div>
      {error && !needsPassword && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-border px-3 py-2 text-[12px] text-danger" data-selectable>
          <span className="flex-1 whitespace-pre-wrap">{error.message}</span>
          <Button size="sm" onClick={retry}>Retry</Button>
        </div>
      )}
      {needsPassword && profile && <UnlockDialog open profile={profile} onOpenChange={() => {}} onUnlocked={retry} />}
      <PanelGroup direction="vertical" className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        <Panel defaultSize={40} minSize={15}>
          <textarea ref={editor} value={sql} onChange={(e) => setSql(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
            spellCheck={false} placeholder="SELECT …  (⌘↵ to run)"
            className="h-full w-full resize-none bg-bg p-3 font-mono text-[13px] outline-none" />
        </Panel>
        <PanelResizeHandle className="h-px bg-border data-[resize-handle-state=drag]:bg-accent" />
        <Panel minSize={20} className="overflow-auto">
          {execution && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-3 text-[12px]">
              {execution.error && (
                <div className="rounded-md border border-danger/40 bg-danger/10 p-2 text-danger" data-selectable>
                  <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{execution.error.message}</div>
                  {execution.error.position && (
                    <div className="mt-1 text-[11px]">
                      Line {execution.error.position.line + lineOffset}, column {execution.error.position.column}
                      {execution.error.code ? ` · ${execution.error.code}` : ""}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
              {execution.resultSets.map((set) => (
                <div key={set.index} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  <ResultTable set={set} />
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted">
                    <span>{set.rows.length.toLocaleString()} rows{set.hasMore ? " loaded" : ""}</span>
                    {set.hasMore && execution.status === "paused" && <Button size="sm" onClick={loadMore}>Load {PAGE} more</Button>}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              {execution.affected.map((a) => <div key={a.index}>{a.rows.toLocaleString()} rows affected</div>)}
              {execution.messages.map((m, i) => (
                <div key={i} className={m.severity === "warning" ? "text-yellow-600 dark:text-yellow-400" : "text-muted"} data-selectable>{m.text}</div>
              ))}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted">
                {execution.status === "running" && <span>Running…</span>}
                {execution.elapsedMs !== null && <span>{execution.elapsedMs} ms</span>}
                {execution.cancelled && <span>Cancelled</span>}
                {execution.inTransaction && <span className="rounded bg-orange-500/20 px-1.5 text-orange-600 dark:text-orange-400">In transaction</span>}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </Panel>
      </PanelGroup>
    </div>
  );
}
```
(Use the same react-resizable-panels export names as Task 12.)

In `src/features/workspace/Workspace.tsx` replace the console placeholder with `<QueryConsole tab={tab} />` (import from `../console/QueryConsole`).

- [ ] **Step 1: PyInstaller spec**

`backend/entry.py` (PyInstaller needs a script, and `__main__.py` uses relative imports):

```python
from dbaguy_backend.__main__ import main

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

`backend/dbaguy-backend.spec`:
```python
# -*- mode: python ; coding: utf-8 -*-
# Build with: .venv/bin/pyinstaller --noconfirm --clean dbaguy-backend.spec
from PyInstaller.utils.hooks import collect_submodules

hiddenimports = [
    *collect_submodules("uvicorn"),
    *collect_submodules("dbaguy_backend"),
    "keyring.backends.macOS",
    "psycopg_binary",
    *collect_submodules("psycopg_binary"),
]

a = Analysis(
    ["entry.py"],
    pathex=["."],
    hiddenimports=hiddenimports,
    excludes=["tkinter", "pytest", "PyInstaller"],
    noarchive=False,
)
pyz = PYZ(a.pure)
exe = EXE(
    pyz,
    a.scripts,
    [],
    exclude_binaries=True,
    name="dbaguy-backend",
    console=True,
    target_arch="arm64",
    codesign_identity=None,
)
coll = COLLECT(exe, a.binaries, a.datas, name="dbaguy-backend")
```

`scripts/build-backend.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build the Python sidecar (PyInstaller onedir) and copy it into the Tauri resources.
set -euo pipefail
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
DEST="$ROOT/src-tauri/resources/backend"

cd "$ROOT/backend"
poetry install --no-interaction
rm -rf build dist
.venv/bin/pyinstaller --noconfirm --clean dbaguy-backend.spec

find "$DEST" -mindepth 1 ! -name .gitkeep -exec rm -rf {} +
cp -R dist/dbaguy-backend/. "$DEST/"
echo "Backend bundled into $DEST"
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Verify the frozen backend on its own**

```bash
chmod +x scripts/build-backend.sh && npm run build:backend
cd src-tauri/resources/backend
DBAGUY_TOKEN=t DBAGUY_PORT=0 DBAGUY_LOG_TO_FILE=false ./dbaguy-backend   # prints {"event":"ready","port":N}
```
In another shell (replace N):
1. `curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer t" http://127.0.0.1:N/system/health` → `{"status":"ok",...}`
2. `curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer t" -H "Content-Type: application/json" -X POST http://127.0.0.1:N/profiles/test -d '{"profile":{"engine":"postgres","name":"pg","host":"127.0.0.1","port":54317,"database":"dbaguy","username":"dbaguy","ssl":{"mode":"disable"}},"secrets":{"password":"dbaguy"}}'` → `"ok":true` (proves psycopg's binary libpq is bundled).
3. A `uuid` column through a session would hit the known "cannot import name _uuid" issue — check with `/profiles/test` plus a quick WebSocket query is optional; if `import uuid` fails in the frozen app, add `"_uuid"` to `hiddenimports`.
Stop the process with Ctrl-C.

- [ ] **Step 3: Build and verify the app bundle**

```bash
npm run build:app
APP="src-tauri/target/release/bundle/macos/DbaGuy.app"
ls "$APP/Contents/Resources/backend/dbaguy-backend"
codesign --verify --deep --strict "$APP" && echo "signature ok"
```
If the bundled backend is broken (missing symlinks inside `_internal`, or `codesign --verify` fails), fix the bundle after `tauri build` with a step in `package.json` `build:app`:
`rm -rf "$APP/Contents/Resources/backend" && ditto src-tauri/resources/backend "$APP/Contents/Resources/backend" && codesign --force --deep -s - "$APP"`, then re-verify.

`scripts/measure-startup.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Launch DbaGuy.app, measure time until the UI's first health check reaches the backend, report memory, quit.
set -euo pipefail
APP="${1:-src-tauri/target/release/bundle/macos/DbaGuy.app}"
LOG="$HOME/Library/Logs/DbaGuy/backend.log"
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$LOG")" && touch "$LOG"
before=$(wc -l < "$LOG")
start=$(python3 -c 'import time; print(time.time())')
open -n "$APP"
for _ in $(seq 1 300); do
  if tail -n +"$((before + 1))" "$LOG" | grep -q "Health check (origin=tauri://localhost)"; then break; fi
  sleep 0.05
done
end=$(python3 -c 'import time; print(time.time())')
python3 -c "print(f'cold start to first API call: {($end - $start):.2f}s')"
sleep 3
ps -axo rss=,comm= | awk '/DbaGuy|dbaguy-backend|WebKit.WebContent|WebKit.Networking|WebKit.GPU/ {sum += $1; print} END {printf "total RSS: %.0f MB\n", sum/1024}'
osascript -e 'quit app "DbaGuy"'
sleep 4
if pgrep -f "Contents/Resources/backend/dbaguy-backend" >/dev/null; then echo "LEFTOVER BACKEND PROCESS"; exit 1; fi
echo "backend exited with the app"
```
(The WebKit processes may include other apps' web views; treat the total as an upper bound and note it.)

Run: `chmod +x scripts/measure-startup.sh && scripts/measure-startup.sh`
Expected: prints the cold-start time and memory, then "backend exited with the app". Record both numbers for Task 16. Targets: ≤ 2 s, ≤ 300 MB; missing them is not a blocker for this task — record and move on.

Also open the built app manually once (`open -n "$APP"`), create a SQLite profile in the real app, run a query, quit — then confirm the Keychain prompt behaviour for a saved password (one "Allow" prompt is expected and acceptable).

- [ ] **Step 2: Update CLAUDE.md**

Add these sections (keep the existing ones; update the progress table: ① → "完成"):
```markdown
## Repo 結構

- `backend/dbaguy_backend/`：Python sidecar
  - `domain/`：pydantic 模型（profile、metadata、WS 執行協定）
  - `adapters/`：`base.py` 是 5 種 DB 共用的介面；每個 engine 一個子套件，在 `container.default_registry()` 註冊
  - `sessions/worker.py`：每個 session 一條連線、一個執行緒；分頁、暫停、取消都在這裡通用處理
  - `services/`：profile、連線（含 SSH tunnel）、metadata
  - `api/`：FastAPI routers；WS 在 `api/sessions.py`
- `src/`：React 前端；API 型別由 `npm run gen:api` 從 OpenAPI 產生（`src/lib/api/schema.d.ts`，不要手改）
- `src-tauri/`：Rust 外殼，只負責視窗與 sidecar 生命週期（`src/backend.rs`）
- `dev/`：測試 DB（Docker）與 seed

## 開發指令

| 用途 | 指令 |
|---|---|
| 啟動測試 DB | `docker compose -f dev/docker-compose.yml up -d --build --wait` |
| Backend 測試 | `cd backend && .venv/bin/pytest -q` |
| 開發（Tauri） | `scripts/dev.sh` |
| 開發（瀏覽器，給 Claude 用內建瀏覽器驗證） | `scripts/dev.sh --web` 後開 `http://localhost:1420` |
| 重新產生 API 型別 | `npm run gen:api` |
| 型別檢查／build | `npm run typecheck` / `npm run build` |
| 打包 `.app` | `npm run build:app` |
| 量測啟動時間與記憶體 | `scripts/measure-startup.sh` |

測試 DB：PG `127.0.0.1:54317`（dbaguy/dbaguy，另有 `analytics` database）；SSH bastion `127.0.0.1:2222`（dev/dev，可連到 `postgres:5432`）。

## 架構重點

- 新增 engine：在 `adapters/<engine>/` 實作 `DatabaseAdapter` / `DatabaseConnection` / `StatementResult`，到 `container.default_registry()` 註冊；分頁、取消、事件都不用重寫。
- WS 執行協定：`domain/execution.py`。結果分頁暫停時佔住連線；新指令會先 discard。
- PG 採 text-first 載入（只有 bool/int2/int4/float/bytea 用原生型別），大數字與 JSON 不失真。
- 密碼只在 Keychain；`save_password=false` 的連線用 `/profiles/{id}/unlock` 暫存在記憶體。
```
